```vba
            If dictResDelay.Exists(key) Then
                resDelays(idx) = dictResDelay(key)
            Else
                resDelays(idx) = 0
            End If
            idx = idx + 1
        Next key
        
        ' Сортировка по убыванию задержек
        Dim m As Long, n As Long
        Dim tempName As String, tempDelay As Double
        For m = 1 To resCount - 1
            For n = m + 1 To resCount
                If resDelays(n) > resDelays(m) Then
                    tempDelay = resDelays(m)
                    resDelays(m) = resDelays(n)
                    resDelays(n) = tempDelay
                    
                    tempName = resNames(m)
                    resNames(m) = resNames(n)
                    resNames(n) = tempName
                End If
            Next n
        Next m
        
        Dim rowIdx As Long
        rowIdx = startRowRes + 1
        For idx = 1 To resCount
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 1).Value = resNames(idx)
            
            Dim totalBusy As Double
            totalBusy = 0
            If dictResTotal.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then totalBusy = dictResTotal(resNames(idx))
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 2).Value = totalBusy
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 3).Value = resDelays(idx)
            rowIdx = rowIdx + 1
        Next idx
        
        With wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":C" & (rowIdx - 1))
            .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
            .Columns.AutoFit
        End With
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":C" & startRowRes).Font.Bold = True
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":C" & startRowRes).Interior.Color = RGB(220, 240, 200)
    End If
    
    wsRes.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub
```

### Что изменилось:

**Структура данных (лист "Данные"):**
- **Столбец A**: Наименование операции
- **Столбец B**: Время поступления
- **Столбец C**: Элементы в формате `Эл1:5:10,Эл2:6:11,Эл3:6:11`
  - Элементы разделены запятой
  - Для каждого: `Имя:Длительность_без_задержки:Длительность_с_задержкой`
- **Столбец D**: Приоритет

**Выходная форма (лист "Результаты"):**
- Порядок столбцов: Наименование → Время поступл. → **Начало обслуж.** → **Время окончания** → Факт. длительность → Задержка → Элементы вызвавшие задержку

**Логика:**
1. Первый элемент в списке автоматически становится каналом
2. При наличии задержки к первому элементу (каналу) применяется длительность "с задержкой", остальные элементы используют длительность "без задержки"
3. Общая длительность = сумма длительностей всех элементов

**Пример данных:**
```
Операция1 | 0 | Эл1:5:10,Эл2:6:11,Эл3:7:12 | 2
Операция2 | 8 | Эл1:5:10,Эл4:8:13          | 1
```

### USER
что тут что?

### ASSISTANT
Давайте разберу структуру кода по частям:

## **1. Константы (настройки столбцов)**
```vba
Const COL_NAME As Integer = 1             ' Столбец A - имя операции
Const COL_ARRIVAL As Integer = 2          ' Столбец B - время поступления
Const COL_ELEMENTS As Integer = 3         ' Столбец C - элементы с длительностями
Const COL_PRIORITY As Integer = 4         ' Столбец D - приоритет
```

## **2. Типы данных**

**TElement** - описывает один элемент:
- `Name` - имя (например, "Эл1")
- `DurationNoDelay` - длительность без задержки (первое число после двоеточия)
- `DurationWithDelay` - длительность с задержкой (второе число)

**TRequest** - описывает заявку:
- `Elements()` - массив элементов
- `InputChannel` - канал (первый элемент из списка)
- `ArrivalTime` - время поступления
- `StartTime/EndTime` - когда начала/закончила обслуживание
- `DelayCausedBy` - какие элементы вызвали задержку

## **3. Основные процедуры**

### **RunSimulation** - главная процедура
- Читает данные из Excel
- Запускает цикл моделирования
- Выводит результаты

### **ParseElements** - разбирает строку элементов
Пример: `"Эл1:5:10,Эл2:6:11"` превращает в:
- Элемент 1: имя="Эл1", без_задержки=5, с_задержкой=10
- Элемент 2: имя="Эл2", без_задержки=6, с_задержкой=11

### **AreResourcesFree** - проверяет, свободны ли все элементы
### **UpdateResources** - помечает элементы как занятые
### **GetEarliestResourceRelease** - находит, когда освободится первый занятый элемент

### **OutputResults** - выводит две таблицы:
1. **Протокол**: кто, когда пришел, когда начал, когда закончил, задержка
2. **Статистика элементов**: сколько времени каждый был занят, сколько задержек вызвал

## **4. Как работает логика**

1. **Канал = первый элемент** в списке (например, "Эл1" в "Эл1:5:10,Эл2:6:11")
2. **Если задержки нет**: все элементы используют первое время (5, 6, 7...)
3. **Если задержка есть**: первый элемент использует второе время (10), остальные - первое (6, 7...)
4. **Задержка** = время начала - время поступления

## **Пример данных в Excel:**

| A | B | C | D |
|---|---|---|---|
| Операция1 | 0 | Эл1:5:10,Эл2:6:11 | 2 |
| Операция2 | 8 | Эл1:5:10,Эл3:7:12 | 1 |

**Что нужно сделать:**
1. Создайте лист "Данные"
2. Заполните столбцы A, B, C, D как показано выше
3. Запустите макрос `RunSimulation`
4. Результаты появятся на листе "Результаты"

Нужна помощь с чем-то конкретным?

### USER
в листе данных столбцы b и d что обозначают

### ASSISTANT
В текущей версии кода (с новым форматом данных) столбцы B и D означают следующее:

### **Столбец B — Время поступления в систему**
Это момент модельного времени, когда заявка появляется в системе и становится доступной для обработки.
*   **Пример:** Если заявка 1 пришла в момент `0`, а заявка 2 в момент `5`, то заявка 2 не может начать обслуживаться раньше времени `5`, даже если все ресурсы свободны.
*   *Важно:* Это числовое значение (например, 0, 1.5, 10, 25).

### **Столбец D — Приоритет**
Это значение, которое влияет на то, насколько "заблаговременно" заявка начинает бронировать ресурсы и насколько высоко она стоит в очереди относительно других каналов.
*   **Как работает:** Чем **больше** значение приоритета, тем выше приоритет заявки. В формуле расчета очереди используется как `(Время поступления - Приоритет)`. То есть, заявка с большим приоритетом математически считается "более старой" и gets обслуживание раньше (если это разные каналы).
*   *Важно:* Внутри одного канала (одного первого элемента) заявки всё равно обслуживаются строго по порядку поступления (FIFO), как вы и просили в самом начале. Приоритет работает только при конкуренции **разных** каналов за одни и те же ресурсы.

---

**Итоговая структура листа "Данные" сейчас:**
*   **Столбец A:** Наименование операции (текст)
*   **Столбец B:** Время поступления (число)
*   **Столбец C:** Элементы (текст в формате `Эл1:5:10,Эл2:6:11`)
*   **Столбец D:** Приоритет (число)

### USER
Ok

### ASSISTANT
Отлично! 

Если в процессе тестирования возникнут вопросы, ошибки или потребуется доработать модель (например, добавить графики загрузки, изменить логику или выгрузить данные в другой формат) — просто напишите.

Удачи с моделированием!

### USER
В результаты перенести столбец элементы

### ASSISTANT
Понял, добавлю столбец "Элементы" (полный список элементов заявки) в таблицу результатов. Размещу его сразу после "Наименование" для логичности.

Вот полный обновленный код:

```vba
Option Explicit

' --- НАСТРОЙКИ СТОЛБЦОВ ИСХОДНЫХ ДАННЫХ ---
Const COL_NAME As Integer = 1             ' A: Наименование операции
Const COL_ARRIVAL As Integer = 2          ' B: Время поступления в систему
Const COL_ELEMENTS As Integer = 3         ' C: Элементы с длительностями (формат: Эл1:5:10,Эл2:6:11)
Const COL_PRIORITY As Integer = 4         ' D: Приоритет

Const SHEET_DATA As String = "Данные"
Const SHEET_RESULT As String = "Результаты"

Type TElement
    Name As String
    DurationNoDelay As Double
    DurationWithDelay As Double
End Type

Type TRequest
    ID As Long
    Name As String
    OriginalElements As String             ' Исходная строка элементов
    Elements() As TElement
    ElementCount As Long
    InputChannel As String                 ' Первый элемент = канал
    Priority As Double
    ArrivalTime As Double
    StartTime As Double
    EndTime As Double
    ActualDuration As Double
    DelayCausedBy As String
    Status As String
End Type

Sub RunSimulation()
    Dim wsData As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsData = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_DATA)
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If wsData Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "Лист '" & SHEET_DATA & "' не найден!", vbCritical
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' 1. Чтение данных
    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = wsData.Cells(wsData.Rows.Count, COL_NAME).End(xlUp).Row
    
    If lastRow < 2 Then
        MsgBox "Нет данных для моделирования (начните со 2-й строки).", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim requests() As TRequest
    ReDim requests(1 To lastRow - 1)
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        requests(i - 1).ID = i - 1
        requests(i - 1).Name = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_NAME).Value)
        
        ' Сохраняем исходную строку элементов
        Dim elementsStr As String
        elementsStr = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ELEMENTS).Value)
        requests(i - 1).OriginalElements = elementsStr
        
        ' Парсинг элементов
        ParseElements elementsStr, requests(i - 1)
        
        ' Первый элемент - это канал
        If requests(i - 1).ElementCount > 0 Then
            requests(i - 1).InputChannel = requests(i - 1).Elements(0).Name
        Else
            requests(i - 1).InputChannel = "Без_канала"
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).Priority = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_PRIORITY).Value)
        
        If IsNumeric(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) And Not IsEmpty(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) Then
            requests(i - 1).ArrivalTime = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value)
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка в строке " & i & ": отсутствует или некорректно указано 'Время поступления' (Столбец B).", vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).ActualDuration = 0
        requests(i - 1).DelayCausedBy = ""
        requests(i - 1).StartTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).EndTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).Status = "New"
    Next i

    ' 2. Инициализация переменных модели
    Dim currentTime As Double
    currentTime = 0
    
    Dim dictResources As Object
    Set dictResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResTotalTime As Object
    Set dictResTotalTime = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResDelayCaused As Object
    Set dictResDelayCaused = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 

    Dim allCompleted As Boolean
    Dim processed As Boolean
    Dim nextTime As Double
    Dim req As Long
    
    ' 3. Цикл моделирования
    Do
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime <= currentTime Then
                requests(req).Status = "Waiting"
            End If
        Next req

        allCompleted = True
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status <> "Completed" Then
                allCompleted = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next req
        If allCompleted Then Exit Do

        processed = False
        
        Dim waitingCount As Long
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then waitingCount = waitingCount + 1
        Next req

        If waitingCount > 0 Then
            Dim dictChannelSums As Object
            Set dictChannelSums = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            
            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim ch As String
                    ch = Trim(CStr(requests(req).InputChannel))
                    If ch = "" Then ch = "Без_канала"
                    
                    If Not dictChannelSums.Exists(ch) Then
                        dictChannelSums.Add ch, 0#
                    End If
                    dictChannelSums(ch) = dictChannelSums(ch) + (currentTime - (requests(req).ArrivalTime - requests(req).Priority))
                End If
            Next req

            If dictChannelSums.Count > 0 Then
                Dim channels() As Variant
                ReDim channels(1 To dictChannelSums.Count)
                Dim k As Long, key As Variant
                k = 1
                For Each key In dictChannelSums.Keys
                    channels(k) = key
                    k = k + 1
                Next key

                Dim j As Long, tempCh As Variant
                For i = 1 To UBound(channels) - 1
                    For j = i + 1 To UBound(channels)
                        If dictChannelSums(channels(j)) > dictChannelSums(channels(i)) Then
                            tempCh = channels(i)
                            channels(i) = channels(j)
                            channels(j) = tempCh
                        End If
                    Next j
                Next i

                For i = 1 To UBound(channels)
                    Dim bestCh As String
                    bestCh = channels(i)

                    Dim headReqIdx As Long
                    headReqIdx = -1
                    Dim minArr As Double
                    minArr = 999999999#

                    For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                        If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" And Trim(CStr(requests(req).InputChannel)) = bestCh Then
                            If requests(req).ArrivalTime < minArr Then
                                minArr = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                                headReqIdx = req
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next req

                    If headReqIdx <> -1 Then
                        If AreResourcesFree(requests(headReqIdx), currentTime, dictResources) Then
                            requests(headReqIdx).StartTime = currentTime
                            
                            Dim isDelayed As Boolean
                            isDelayed = (requests(headReqIdx).StartTime > requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
                            
                            ' Расчет фактической длительности
                            Dim totalDuration As Double
                            totalDuration = 0
                            
                            Dim idx As Long
                            For idx = 0 To requests(headReqIdx).ElementCount - 1
                                Dim elemDuration As Double
                                If isDelayed And idx = 0 Then
                                    ' Для первого элемента (канала) при задержке используем длительность с задержкой
                                    elemDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(idx).DurationWithDelay
                                Else
                                    ' Для остальных элементов или без задержки - без задержки
                                    elemDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(idx).DurationNoDelay
                                End If
                                totalDuration = totalDuration + elemDuration
                            Next idx
                            
                            requests(headReqIdx).ActualDuration = totalDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).EndTime = currentTime + totalDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).Status = "Completed"
                            
                            ' === ОПРЕДЕЛЕНИЕ ЭЛЕМЕНТОВ, ВЫЗВАВШИХ ЗАДЕРЖКУ ===
                            If isDelayed Then
                                Dim maxFreeTime As Double
                                maxFreeTime = 0
                                Dim allBottlenecks As String
                                allBottlenecks = ""
                                
                                ' Находим максимальное время освобождения среди всех элементов
                                For idx = 0 To requests(headReqIdx).ElementCount - 1
                                    Dim resName As String
                                    resName = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(idx).Name
                                    Dim freeTime As Double
                                    freeTime = dictResources(resName)
                                    If freeTime > maxFreeTime Then
                                        maxFreeTime = freeTime
                                    End If
                                Next idx
                                
                                ' Вычисляем величину задержки
                                Dim delayAmount As Double
                                delayAmount = requests(headReqIdx).StartTime - requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime
                                
                                ' Собираем все элементы, которые освободились в момент maxFreeTime
                                For idx = 0 To requests(headReqIdx).ElementCount - 1
                                    Dim resName2 As String
                                    resName2 = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(idx).Name
                                    Dim freeTime2 As Double
                                    freeTime2 = dictResources(resName2)
                                    
                                    If Abs(freeTime2 - maxFreeTime) < 0.0001 Then
                                        If allBottlenecks = "" Then
                                            allBottlenecks = resName2
                                        Else
                                            allBottlenecks = allBottlenecks & ", " & resName2
                                        End If
                                        
                                        If Not dictResDelayCaused.Exists(resName2) Then
                                            dictResDelayCaused.Add resName2, 0#
                                        End If
                                        dictResDelayCaused(resName2) = dictResDelayCaused(resName2) + delayAmount
                                    End If
                                Next idx
                                
                                requests(headReqIdx).DelayCausedBy = allBottlenecks
                            End If
                            ' =====================================================
                            
                            UpdateResources requests(headReqIdx), dictResources, dictResTotalTime
                            
                            processed = True
                            Exit For 
                        End If
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
        End If

        If Not processed Then
            nextTime = 999999999#

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime > currentTime Then
                    If requests(req).ArrivalTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim relTime As Double
                    relTime = GetEarliestResourceRelease(requests(req), currentTime, dictResources)
                    If relTime > currentTime And relTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = relTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            If nextTime = 999999999# Then Exit Do
            
            currentTime = nextTime
        End If
    Loop

    OutputResults requests, dictResTotalTime, dictResDelayCaused
    
    MsgBox "Моделирование завершено успешно!", vbInformation
End Sub

Sub ParseElements(elementsStr As String, ByRef req As TRequest)
    Dim arr() As String
    arr = Split(elementsStr, ",")
    
    Dim count As Long
    count = UBound(arr) - LBound(arr) + 1
    
    ReDim req.Elements(0 To count - 1)
    req.ElementCount = count
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr)
        Dim part As String
        part = Trim(arr(i))
        
        Dim parts() As String
        parts = Split(part, ":")
        
        If UBound(parts) >= 2 Then
            req.Elements(i).Name = Trim(parts(0))
            req.Elements(i).DurationNoDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(1)))
            req.Elements(i).DurationWithDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(2)))
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка парсинга элемента: " & part, vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

Function AreResourcesFree(req As TRequest, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long
    AreResourcesFree = True
    
    For i = 0 To req.ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            dictRes.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        
        If dictRes(resName) > currentTime Then
            AreResourcesFree = False
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

Sub UpdateResources(req As TRequest, dictRes As Object, dictResTotal As Object)
    Dim i As Long
    
    For i = 0 To req.ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            dictRes.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        
        If req.EndTime > dictRes(resName) Then
            dictRes(resName) = req.EndTime
        End If
        
        ' Определяем длительность для этого элемента
        Dim duration As Double
        Dim isDelayed As Boolean
        isDelayed = (req.StartTime > req.ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
        
        If isDelayed And i = 0 Then
            duration = req.Elements(i).DurationWithDelay
        Else
            duration = req.Elements(i).DurationNoDelay
        End If
        
        If Not dictResTotal.Exists(resName) Then
            dictResTotal.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        dictResTotal(resName) = dictResTotal(resName) + duration
    Next i
End Sub

Function GetEarliestResourceRelease(req As TRequest, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    Dim i As Long
    Dim earliest As Double
    earliest = 999999999#
    
    For i = 0 To req.ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Elements(i).Name
        
        If dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim busyUntil As Double
            busyUntil = dictRes(resName)
            If busyUntil > currentTime And busyUntil < earliest Then
                earliest = busyUntil
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    GetEarliestResourceRelease = earliest
End Function

Sub OutputResults(requests() As TRequest, dictResTotal As Object, dictResDelay As Object)
    Dim wsRes As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_RESULT)
    If wsRes Is Nothing Then
        Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        wsRes.Name = SHEET_RESULT
    Else
        wsRes.Cells.Clear
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0

    ' === ТАБЛИЦА 1: Протокол модели ===
    wsRes.Cells(1, 1).Value = "Наименование"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 2).Value = "Элементы"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 3).Value = "Время поступл."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 4).Value = "Начало обслуж."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 5).Value = "Время окончания"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 6).Value = "Факт. длительность"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 7).Value = "Задержка"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 8).Value = "Элементы, вызвавшие задержку"
    
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To UBound(requests)
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 1).Value = requests(r).Name
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 2).Value = requests(r).OriginalElements
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 3).Value = requests(r).ArrivalTime
        
        If requests(r).StartTime >= 0 Then
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 4).Value = requests(r).StartTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 5).Value = requests(r).EndTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 6).Value = requests(r).ActualDuration
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 7).Value = requests(r).StartTime - requests(r).ArrivalTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 8).Value = requests(r).DelayCausedBy
        Else
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 4).Value = "Ошибка"
        End If
    Next r
    
    With wsRes.Range("A1:H" & UBound(requests) + 1)
        .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Columns.AutoFit
    End With
    wsRes.Range("A1:H1").Font.Bold = True
    wsRes.Range("A1:H1").Interior.Color = RGB(200, 220, 240)

    ' === ТАБЛИЦА 2: Загрузка и задержки элементов (ОТСОРТИРОВАННАЯ) ===
    Dim startRowRes As Long
    startRowRes = UBound(requests) + 4
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 1).Value = "Элемент"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 2).Value = "Суммарное время занятости"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 3).Value = "Суммарная задержка, вызванная элементом"
    
    Dim allResources As Object
    Set allResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In dictResTotal.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key
    For Each key In dictResDelay.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key

    Dim resCount As Long
    resCount = allResources.Count
    
    If resCount > 0 Then
        Dim resNames() As String
        Dim resDelays() As Double
        ReDim resNames(1 To resCount)
        ReDim resDelays(1 To resCount)
        
        Dim idx As Long
        idx = 1
        For Each key In allResources.Keys
            resNames(idx) = key
            If dictResDelay.Exists(key) Then
                resDelays(idx) = dictResDelay(key)
            Else
                resDelays(idx) = 0
            End If
            idx = idx + 1
        Next key
        
        ' Сортировка по убыванию задержек
        Dim m As Long, n As Long
        Dim tempName As String, tempDelay As Double
        For m = 1 To resCount - 1
            For n = m + 1 To resCount
                If resDelays(n) > resDelays(m) Then
                    tempDelay = resDelays(m)
                    resDelays(m) = resDelays(n)
                    resDelays(n) = tempDelay
                    
                    tempName = resNames(m)
                    resNames(m) = resNames(n)
                    resNames(n) = tempName
                End If
            Next n
        Next m
        
        Dim rowIdx As Long
        rowIdx = startRowRes + 1
        For idx = 1 To resCount
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 1).Value = resNames(idx)
            
            Dim totalBusy As Double
            totalBusy = 0
            If dictResTotal.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then totalBusy = dictResTotal(resNames(idx))
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 2).Value = totalBusy
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 3).Value = resDelays(idx)
            rowIdx = rowIdx + 1
        Next idx
        
        With wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":C" & (rowIdx - 1))
            .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
            .Columns.AutoFit
        End With
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":C" & startRowRes).Font.Bold = True
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":C" & startRowRes).Interior.Color = RGB(220, 240, 200)
    End If
    
    wsRes.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub
```

### Что изменилось:

1. **Добавлено поле `OriginalElements`** в тип `TRequest` для хранения исходной строки элементов
2. **При чтении данных** сохраняется полная строка элементов: `requests(i - 1).OriginalElements = elementsStr`
3. **В таблице результатов** добавлен столбец "Элементы" (столбец B) сразу после "Наименование"
4. **Порядок столбцов теперь:**
   - A: Наименование
   - **B: Элементы** (новый)
   - C: Время поступл.
   - D: Начало обслуж.
   - E: Время окончания
   - F: Факт. длительность
   - G: Задержка
   - H: Элементы, вызвавшие задержку

Теперь в результатах видно полный список элементов для каждой операции в исходном формате (например, "Эл1:5:10,Эл2:6:11").

### USER
Вот результат. Проанализируем. Первая операция пришла в 0, обслужилась без задержек в течение 10 минут. Почему, если без задержек должна 5 минут. Вторая пришла в 5 минут. Должна была без задержек начать обслуживаться. Но обслужилась с задержкой. С задержкой для нее время обслуживания 10 мин. Почему фактическое время обслуживания 20 минут? Теперь, если суммарное время задержек по элементу 1 составило 67 минут, то как минимум суммарное время занятия элементов ВхI и ВхII должно быть больше 67 минут, а у нас 45?

### ASSISTANT
Вижу три критические проблемы в логике моделирования:

## **Проблема 1: Неправильный расчет времени обслуживания**

В коде я **суммирую** длительности всех элементов (ВхII:5 + 1:5 = 10 минут), но должно быть **параллельное выполнение** — время обслуживания = время канала (первого элемента).

**Операция 1**: ВхII:5:10,1:5:10 → должно быть 5 минут (время ВхII), а не 10.

## **Проблема 2: Элементы освобождаются в разное время**

Из-за суммирования, элемент "1" освобождается позже канала. Но все элементы должны освобождаться **одновременно** в момент окончания операции.

**Операция 2**: пришла в 5, элемент "1" занят до 10 (из-за операции 1), поэтому ждет. Но факт = 20 вместо 10, потому что я снова суммирую (10+5+5).

## **Проблема 3: Несоответствие задержек и времени занятости**

Задержка 67 минут приписана элементу "1", но он используется параллельно с каналом. На самом деле bottleneck = канал (ВхI или ВхII), потому что именно он определяет время обслуживания.

---

## **Исправленный код:**

```vba
Option Explicit

Const COL_NAME As Integer = 1
Const COL_ARRIVAL As Integer = 2
Const COL_ELEMENTS As Integer = 3
Const COL_PRIORITY As Integer = 4

Const SHEET_DATA As String = "Данные"
Const SHEET_RESULT As String = "Результаты"

Type TElement
    Name As String
    DurationNoDelay As Double
    DurationWithDelay As Double
End Type

Type TRequest
    ID As Long
    Name As String
    OriginalElements As String
    Elements() As TElement
    ElementCount As Long
    InputChannel As String
    Priority As Double
    ArrivalTime As Double
    StartTime As Double
    EndTime As Double
    ActualDuration As Double
    DelayCausedBy As String
    Status As String
End Type

Sub RunSimulation()
    Dim wsData As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsData = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_DATA)
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If wsData Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "Лист '" & SHEET_DATA & "' не найден!", vbCritical
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = wsData.Cells(wsData.Rows.Count, COL_NAME).End(xlUp).Row
    
    If lastRow < 2 Then
        MsgBox "Нет данных для моделирования (начните со 2-й строки).", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim requests() As TRequest
    ReDim requests(1 To lastRow - 1)
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        requests(i - 1).ID = i - 1
        requests(i - 1).Name = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_NAME).Value)
        
        Dim elementsStr As String
        elementsStr = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ELEMENTS).Value)
        requests(i - 1).OriginalElements = elementsStr
        
        ParseElements elementsStr, requests(i - 1)
        
        If requests(i - 1).ElementCount > 0 Then
            requests(i - 1).InputChannel = requests(i - 1).Elements(0).Name
        Else
            requests(i - 1).InputChannel = "Без_канала"
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).Priority = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_PRIORITY).Value)
        
        If IsNumeric(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) And Not IsEmpty(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) Then
            requests(i - 1).ArrivalTime = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value)
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка в строке " & i & ": отсутствует или некорректно указано 'Время поступления' (Столбец B).", vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).ActualDuration = 0
        requests(i - 1).DelayCausedBy = ""
        requests(i - 1).StartTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).EndTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).Status = "New"
    Next i

    Dim currentTime As Double
    currentTime = 0
    
    Dim dictResources As Object
    Set dictResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResTotalTime As Object
    Set dictResTotalTime = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResDelayCaused As Object
    Set dictResDelayCaused = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 

    Dim allCompleted As Boolean
    Dim processed As Boolean
    Dim nextTime As Double
    Dim req As Long
    
    Do
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime <= currentTime Then
                requests(req).Status = "Waiting"
            End If
        Next req

        allCompleted = True
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status <> "Completed" Then
                allCompleted = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next req
        If allCompleted Then Exit Do

        processed = False
        
        Dim waitingCount As Long
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then waitingCount = waitingCount + 1
        Next req

        If waitingCount > 0 Then
            Dim dictChannelSums As Object
            Set dictChannelSums = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            
            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim ch As String
                    ch = Trim(CStr(requests(req).InputChannel))
                    If ch = "" Then ch = "Без_канала"
                    
                    If Not dictChannelSums.Exists(ch) Then
                        dictChannelSums.Add ch, 0#
                    End If
                    dictChannelSums(ch) = dictChannelSums(ch) + (currentTime - (requests(req).ArrivalTime - requests(req).Priority))
                End If
            Next req

            If dictChannelSums.Count > 0 Then
                Dim channels() As Variant
                ReDim channels(1 To dictChannelSums.Count)
                Dim k As Long, key As Variant
                k = 1
                For Each key In dictChannelSums.Keys
                    channels(k) = key
                    k = k + 1
                Next key

                Dim j As Long, tempCh As Variant
                For i = 1 To UBound(channels) - 1
                    For j = i + 1 To UBound(channels)
                        If dictChannelSums(channels(j)) > dictChannelSums(channels(i)) Then
                            tempCh = channels(i)
                            channels(i) = channels(j)
                            channels(j) = tempCh
                        End If
                    Next j
                Next i

                For i = 1 To UBound(channels)
                    Dim bestCh As String
                    bestCh = channels(i)

                    Dim headReqIdx As Long
                    headReqIdx = -1
                    Dim minArr As Double
                    minArr = 999999999#

                    For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                        If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" And Trim(CStr(requests(req).InputChannel)) = bestCh Then
                            If requests(req).ArrivalTime < minArr Then
                                minArr = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                                headReqIdx = req
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next req

                    If headReqIdx <> -1 Then
                        If AreResourcesFree(requests(headReqIdx), currentTime, dictResources) Then
                            requests(headReqIdx).StartTime = currentTime
                            
                            Dim isDelayed As Boolean
                            isDelayed = (requests(headReqIdx).StartTime > requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
                            
                            ' === ИСПРАВЛЕНИЕ: время = время канала (первого элемента) ===
                            Dim channelDuration As Double
                            If isDelayed Then
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
                            Else
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
                            End If
                            
                            requests(headReqIdx).ActualDuration = channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).EndTime = currentTime + channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).Status = "Completed"
                            
                            ' === ОПРЕДЕЛЕНИЕ BOTTLENECK ===
                            If isDelayed Then
                                ' Bottleneck = канал (первый элемент), т.к. все элементы освобождаются одновременно
                                Dim bottleneckRes As String
                                bottleneckRes = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(0).Name
                                
                                Dim delayAmount As Double
                                delayAmount = requests(headReqIdx).StartTime - requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime
                                
                                If Not dictResDelayCaused.Exists(bottleneckRes) Then
                                    dictResDelayCaused.Add bottleneckRes, 0#
                                End If
                                dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) = dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) + delayAmount
                                
                                requests(headReqIdx).DelayCausedBy = bottleneckRes
                            End If
                            
                            UpdateResources requests(headReqIdx), dictResources, dictResTotalTime
                            
                            processed = True
                            Exit For 
                        End If
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
        End If

        If Not processed Then
            nextTime = 999999999#

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime > currentTime Then
                    If requests(req).ArrivalTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim relTime As Double
                    relTime = GetEarliestResourceRelease(requests(req), currentTime, dictResources)
                    If relTime > currentTime And relTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = relTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            If nextTime = 999999999# Then Exit Do
            
            currentTime = nextTime
        End If
    Loop

    OutputResults requests, dictResTotalTime, dictResDelayCaused
    
    MsgBox "Моделирование завершено успешно!", vbInformation
End Sub

Sub ParseElements(elementsStr As String, ByRef req As TRequest)
    Dim arr() As String
    arr = Split(elementsStr, ",")
    
    Dim count As Long
    count = UBound(arr) - LBound(arr) + 1
    
    ReDim req.Elements(0 To count - 1)
    req.ElementCount = count
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr)
        Dim part As String
        part = Trim(arr(i))
        
        Dim parts() As String
        parts = Split(part, ":")
        
        If UBound(parts) >= 2 Then
            req.Elements(i).Name = Trim(parts(0))
            req.Elements(i).DurationNoDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(1)))
            req.Elements(i).DurationWithDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(2)))
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка парсинга элемента: " & part, vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

Function AreResourcesFree(req As TRequest, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long
    AreResourcesFree = True
    
    For i = 0 To req.ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            dictRes.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        
        If dictRes(resName) > currentTime Then
            AreResourcesFree = False
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

Sub UpdateResources(req As TRequest, dictRes As Object, dictResTotal As Object)
    Dim i As Long
    
    ' Все элементы занимаются на одно и то же время (время канала)
    Dim duration As Double
    Dim isDelayed As Boolean
    isDelayed = (req.StartTime > req.ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
    
    If isDelayed Then
        duration = req.Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
    Else
        duration = req.Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
    End If
    
    For i = 0 To req.ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            dictRes.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        
        If req.EndTime > dictRes(resName) Then
            dictRes(resName) = req.EndTime
        End If
        
        If Not dictResTotal.Exists(resName) Then
            dictResTotal.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        dictResTotal(resName) = dictResTotal(resName) + duration
    Next i
End Sub

Function GetEarliestResourceRelease(req As TRequest, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    Dim i As Long
    Dim earliest As Double
    earliest = 999999999#
    
    For i = 0 To req.ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Elements(i).Name
        
        If dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim busyUntil As Double
            busyUntil = dictRes(resName)
            If busyUntil > currentTime And busyUntil < earliest Then
                earliest = busyUntil
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    GetEarliestResourceRelease = earliest
End Function

Sub OutputResults(requests() As TRequest, dictResTotal As Object, dictResDelay As Object)
    Dim wsRes As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_RESULT)
    If wsRes Is Nothing Then
        Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        wsRes.Name = SHEET_RESULT
    Else
        wsRes.Cells.Clear
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0

    wsRes.Cells(1, 1).Value = "Наименование"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 2).Value = "Элементы"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 3).Value = "Время поступл."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 4).Value = "Начало обслуж."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 5).Value = "Время окончания"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 6).Value = "Факт. длительность"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 7).Value = "Задержка"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 8).Value = "Элементы, вызвавшие задержку"
    
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To UBound(requests)
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 1).Value = requests(r).Name
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 2).Value = requests(r).OriginalElements
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 3).Value = requests(r).ArrivalTime
        
        If requests(r).StartTime >= 0 Then
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 4).Value = requests(r).StartTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 5).Value = requests(r).EndTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 6).Value = requests(r).ActualDuration
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 7).Value = requests(r).StartTime - requests(r).ArrivalTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 8).Value = requests(r).DelayCausedBy
        Else
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 4).Value = "Ошибка"
        End If
    Next r
    
    With wsRes.Range("A1:H" & UBound(requests) + 1)
        .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Columns.AutoFit
    End With
    wsRes.Range("A1:H1").Font.Bold = True
    wsRes.Range("A1:H1").Interior.Color = RGB(200, 220, 240)

    Dim startRowRes As Long
    startRowRes = UBound(requests) + 4
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 1).Value = "Элемент"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 2).Value = "Суммарное время занятости"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 3).Value = "Суммарная задержка, вызванная элементом"
    
    Dim allResources As Object
    Set allResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In dictResTotal.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key
    For Each key In dictResDelay.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key

    Dim resCount As Long
    resCount = allResources.Count
    
    If resCount > 0 Then
        Dim resNames() As String
        Dim resDelays() As Double
        ReDim resNames(1 To resCount)
        ReDim resDelays(1 To resCount)
        
        Dim idx As Long
        idx = 1
        For Each key In allResources.Keys
            resNames(idx) = key
            If dictResDelay.Exists(key) Then
                resDelays(idx) = dictResDelay(key)
            Else
                resDelays(idx) = 0
            End If
            idx = idx + 1
        Next key
        
        Dim m As Long, n As Long
        Dim tempName As String, tempDelay As Double
        For m = 1 To resCount - 1
            For n = m + 1 To resCount
                If resDelays(n) > resDelays(m) Then
                    tempDelay = resDelays(m)
                    resDelays(m) = resDelays(n)
                    resDelays(n) = tempDelay
                    
                    tempName = resNames(m)
                    resNames(m) = resNames(n)
                    resNames(n) = tempName
                End If
            Next n
        Next m
        
        Dim rowIdx As Long
        rowIdx = startRowRes + 1
        For idx = 1 To resCount
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 1).Value = resNames(idx)
            
            Dim totalBusy As Double
            totalBusy = 0
            If dictResTotal.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then totalBusy = dictResTotal(resNames(idx))
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 2).Value = totalBusy
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 3).Value = resDelays(idx)
            rowIdx = rowIdx + 1
        Next idx
        
        With wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":C" & (rowIdx - 1))
            .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
            .Columns.AutoFit
        End With
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":C" & startRowRes).Font.Bold = True
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":C" & startRowRes).Interior.Color = RGB(220, 240, 200)
    End If
    
    wsRes.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub
```

### **Что исправлено:**

1. **Время обслуживания = время канала** (первого элемента). Остальные элементы работают параллельно.
2. **Все элементы освобождаются одновременно** в момент `EndTime`.
3. **Bottleneck = канал** (ВхI или ВхII), потому что именно он определяет время обслуживания и создает задержку.
4. **Время занятости всех элементов = время канала** (не сумма).

Теперь для ваших данных:
- Операция 1: 5 минут (ВхII), элемент "1" занят 5 минут
- Операция 2: пришла в 5, элемент "1" свободен, начинает сразу, 5 минут (ВхI)
- Задержка будет приписана каналу (ВхI или ВхII), а не элементу "1"

### USER
Для каждого элемента формируем список ("Элемент i" ("Операция 1" 9 20) ("Операция 2" 21 100) ...)

### ASSISTANT
Понял! Нужно для каждого элемента сформировать детальный список всех операций, которые его использовали, с указанием времени начала и окончания.

Вот полный обновленный код:

```vba
Option Explicit

Const COL_NAME As Integer = 1
Const COL_ARRIVAL As Integer = 2
Const COL_ELEMENTS As Integer = 3
Const COL_PRIORITY As Integer = 4

Const SHEET_DATA As String = "Данные"
Const SHEET_RESULT As String = "Результаты"

Type TElement
    Name As String
    DurationNoDelay As Double
    DurationWithDelay As Double
End Type

Type TElementUsage
    OperationName As String
    StartTime As Double
    EndTime As Double
End Type

Type TRequest
    ID As Long
    Name As String
    OriginalElements As String
    Elements() As TElement
    ElementCount As Long
    InputChannel As String
    Priority As Double
    ArrivalTime As Double
    StartTime As Double
    EndTime As Double
    ActualDuration As Double
    DelayCausedBy As String
    Status As String
End Type

Sub RunSimulation()
    Dim wsData As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsData = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_DATA)
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If wsData Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "Лист '" & SHEET_DATA & "' не найден!", vbCritical
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = wsData.Cells(wsData.Rows.Count, COL_NAME).End(xlUp).Row
    
    If lastRow < 2 Then
        MsgBox "Нет данных для моделирования (начните со 2-й строки).", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim requests() As TRequest
    ReDim requests(1 To lastRow - 1)
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        requests(i - 1).ID = i - 1
        requests(i - 1).Name = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_NAME).Value)
        
        Dim elementsStr As String
        elementsStr = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ELEMENTS).Value)
        requests(i - 1).OriginalElements = elementsStr
        
        ParseElements elementsStr, requests(i - 1)
        
        If requests(i - 1).ElementCount > 0 Then
            requests(i - 1).InputChannel = requests(i - 1).Elements(0).Name
        Else
            requests(i - 1).InputChannel = "Без_канала"
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).Priority = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_PRIORITY).Value)
        
        If IsNumeric(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) And Not IsEmpty(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) Then
            requests(i - 1).ArrivalTime = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value)
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка в строке " & i & ": отсутствует или некорректно указано 'Время поступления' (Столбец B).", vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).ActualDuration = 0
        requests(i - 1).DelayCausedBy = ""
        requests(i - 1).StartTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).EndTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).Status = "New"
    Next i

    Dim currentTime As Double
    currentTime = 0
    
    Dim dictResources As Object
    Set dictResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResTotalTime As Object
    Set dictResTotalTime = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResDelayCaused As Object
    Set dictResDelayCaused = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    ' Словарь для хранения списка использования каждого элемента
    Dim dictElementUsage As Object
    Set dictElementUsage = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")

    Dim allCompleted As Boolean
    Dim processed As Boolean
    Dim nextTime As Double
    Dim req As Long
    
    Do
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime <= currentTime Then
                requests(req).Status = "Waiting"
            End If
        Next req

        allCompleted = True
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status <> "Completed" Then
                allCompleted = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next req
        If allCompleted Then Exit Do

        processed = False
        
        Dim waitingCount As Long
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then waitingCount = waitingCount + 1
        Next req

        If waitingCount > 0 Then
            Dim dictChannelSums As Object
            Set dictChannelSums = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            
            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim ch As String
                    ch = Trim(CStr(requests(req).InputChannel))
                    If ch = "" Then ch = "Без_канала"
                    
                    If Not dictChannelSums.Exists(ch) Then
                        dictChannelSums.Add ch, 0#
                    End If
                    dictChannelSums(ch) = dictChannelSums(ch) + (currentTime - (requests(req).ArrivalTime - requests(req).Priority))
                End If
            Next req

            If dictChannelSums.Count > 0 Then
                Dim channels() As Variant
                ReDim channels(1 To dictChannelSums.Count)
                Dim k As Long, key As Variant
                k = 1
                For Each key In dictChannelSums.Keys
                    channels(k) = key
                    k = k + 1
                Next key

                Dim j As Long, tempCh As Variant
                For i = 1 To UBound(channels) - 1
                    For j = i + 1 To UBound(channels)
                        If dictChannelSums(channels(j)) > dictChannelSums(channels(i)) Then
                            tempCh = channels(i)
                            channels(i) = channels(j)
                            channels(j) = tempCh
                        End If
                    Next j
                Next i

                For i = 1 To UBound(channels)
                    Dim bestCh As String
                    bestCh = channels(i)

                    Dim headReqIdx As Long
                    headReqIdx = -1
                    Dim minArr As Double
                    minArr = 999999999#

                    For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                        If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" And Trim(CStr(requests(req).InputChannel)) = bestCh Then
                            If requests(req).ArrivalTime < minArr Then
                                minArr = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                                headReqIdx = req
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next req

                    If headReqIdx <> -1 Then
                        If AreResourcesFree(requests(headReqIdx), currentTime, dictResources) Then
                            requests(headReqIdx).StartTime = currentTime
                            
                            Dim isDelayed As Boolean
                            isDelayed = (requests(headReqIdx).StartTime > requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
                            
                            Dim channelDuration As Double
                            If isDelayed Then
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
                            Else
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
                            End If
                            
                            requests(headReqIdx).ActualDuration = channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).EndTime = currentTime + channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).Status = "Completed"
                            
                            If isDelayed Then
                                Dim bottleneckRes As String
                                bottleneckRes = requests(headReqIdx).Elements(0).Name
                                
                                Dim delayAmount As Double
                                delayAmount = requests(headReqIdx).StartTime - requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime
                                
                                If Not dictResDelayCaused.Exists(bottleneckRes) Then
                                    dictResDelayCaused.Add bottleneckRes, 0#
                                End If
                                dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) = dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) + delayAmount
                                
                                requests(headReqIdx).DelayCausedBy = bottleneckRes
                            End If
                            
                            ' === ЗАПИСЬ ИСПОЛЬЗОВАНИЯ ЭЛЕМЕНТОВ ===
                            UpdateResources requests(headReqIdx), dictResources, dictResTotalTime, dictElementUsage
                            ' =========================================
                            
                            processed = True
                            Exit For 
                        End If
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
        End If

        If Not processed Then
            nextTime = 999999999#

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime > currentTime Then
                    If requests(req).ArrivalTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim relTime As Double
                    relTime = GetEarliestResourceRelease(requests(req), currentTime, dictResources)
                    If relTime > currentTime And relTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = relTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            If nextTime = 999999999# Then Exit Do
            
            currentTime = nextTime
        End If
    Loop

    OutputResults requests, dictResTotalTime, dictResDelayCaused, dictElementUsage
    
    MsgBox "Моделирование завершено успешно!", vbInformation
End Sub

Sub ParseElements(elementsStr As String, ByRef req As TRequest)
    Dim arr() As String
    arr = Split(elementsStr, ",")
    
    Dim count As Long
    count = UBound(arr) - LBound(arr) + 1
    
    ReDim req.Elements(0 To count - 1)
    req.ElementCount = count
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr)
        Dim part As String
        part = Trim(arr(i))
        
        Dim parts() As String
        parts = Split(part, ":")
        
        If UBound(parts) >= 2 Then
            req.Elements(i).Name = Trim(parts(0))
            req.Elements(i).DurationNoDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(1)))
            req.Elements(i).DurationWithDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(2)))
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка парсинга элемента: " & part, vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

Function AreResourcesFree(req As TRequest, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long
    AreResourcesFree = True
    
    For i = 0 To req.ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            dictRes.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        
        If dictRes(resName) > currentTime Then
            AreResourcesFree = False
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

Sub UpdateResources(req As TRequest, dictRes As Object, dictResTotal As Object, dictElementUsage As Object)
    Dim i As Long
    
    Dim duration As Double
    Dim isDelayed As Boolean
    isDelayed = (req.StartTime > req.ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
    
    If isDelayed Then
        duration = req.Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
    Else
        duration = req.Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
    End If
    
    For i = 0 To req.ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            dictRes.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        
        If req.EndTime > dictRes(resName) Then
            dictRes(resName) = req.EndTime
        End If
        
        If Not dictResTotal.Exists(resName) Then
            dictResTotal.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        dictResTotal(resName) = dictResTotal(resName) + duration
        
        ' === ДОБАВЛЕНИЕ В СПИСОК ИСПОЛЬЗОВАНИЯ ===
        If Not dictElementUsage.Exists(resName) Then
            dictElementUsage.Add resName, CreateObject("System.Collections.ArrayList")
        End If
        
        Dim usageList As Object
        Set usageList = dictElementUsage(resName)
        
        ' Создаем запись об использовании
        Dim usageEntry As String
        usageEntry = "(""" & req.Name & """ " & req.StartTime & " " & req.EndTime & ")"
        usageList.Add usageEntry
        ' ==========================================
    Next i
End Sub

Function GetEarliestResourceRelease(req As TRequest, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    Dim i As Long
    Dim earliest As Double
    earliest = 999999999#
    
    For i = 0 To req.ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Elements(i).Name
        
        If dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim busyUntil As Double
            busyUntil = dictRes(resName)
            If busyUntil > currentTime And busyUntil < earliest Then
                earliest = busyUntil
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    GetEarliestResourceRelease = earliest
End Function

Sub OutputResults(requests() As TRequest, dictResTotal As Object, dictResDelay As Object, dictElementUsage As Object)
    Dim wsRes As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_RESULT)
    If wsRes Is Nothing Then
        Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        wsRes.Name = SHEET_RESULT
    Else
        wsRes.Cells.Clear
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0

    wsRes.Cells(1, 1).Value = "Наименование"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 2).Value = "Элементы"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 3).Value = "Время поступл."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 4).Value = "Начало обслуж."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 5).Value = "Время окончания"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 6).Value = "Факт. длительность"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 7).Value = "Задержка"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 8).Value = "Элементы, вызвавшие задержку"
    
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To UBound(requests)
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 1).Value = requests(r).Name
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 2).Value = requests(r).OriginalElements
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 3).Value = requests(r).ArrivalTime
        
        If requests(r).StartTime >= 0 Then
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 4).Value = requests(r).StartTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 5).Value = requests(r).EndTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 6).Value = requests(r).ActualDuration
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 7).Value = requests(r).StartTime - requests(r).ArrivalTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 8).Value = requests(r).DelayCausedBy
        Else
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 4).Value = "Ошибка"
        End If
    Next r
    
    With wsRes.Range("A1:H" & UBound(requests) + 1)
        .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Columns.AutoFit
    End With
    wsRes.Range("A1:H1").Font.Bold = True
    wsRes.Range("A1:H1").Interior.Color = RGB(200, 220, 240)

    ' === ТАБЛИЦА 2: Загрузка и задержки элементов ===
    Dim startRowRes As Long
    startRowRes = UBound(requests) + 4
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 1).Value = "Элемент"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 2).Value = "Суммарное время занятости"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 3).Value = "Суммарная задержка, вызванная элементом"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 4).Value = "Список операций (Операция Начало Конец)"
    
    Dim allResources As Object
    Set allResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In dictResTotal.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key
    For Each key In dictResDelay.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key

    Dim resCount As Long
    resCount = allResources.Count
    
    If resCount > 0 Then
        Dim resNames() As String
        Dim resDelays() As Double
        ReDim resNames(1 To resCount)
        ReDim resDelays(1 To resCount)
        
        Dim idx As Long
        idx = 1
        For Each key In allResources.Keys
            resNames(idx) = key
            If dictResDelay.Exists(key) Then
                resDelays(idx) = dictResDelay(key)
            Else
                resDelays(idx) = 0
            End If
            idx = idx + 1
        Next key
        
        Dim m As Long, n As Long
        Dim tempName As String, tempDelay As Double
        For m = 1 To resCount - 1
            For n = m + 1 To resCount
                If resDelays(n) > resDelays(m) Then
                    tempDelay = resDelays(m)
                    resDelays(m) = resDelays(n)
                    resDelays(n) = tempDelay
                    
                    tempName = resNames(m)
                    resNames(m) = resNames(n)
                    resNames(n) = tempName
                End If
            Next n
        Next m
        
        Dim rowIdx As Long
        rowIdx = startRowRes + 1
        For idx = 1 To resCount
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 1).Value = resNames(idx)
            
            Dim totalBusy As Double
            totalBusy = 0
            If dictResTotal.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then totalBusy = dictResTotal(resNames(idx))
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 2).Value = totalBusy
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 3).Value = resDelays(idx)
            
            ' Формируем строку со списком операций
            Dim usageStr As String
            usageStr = """" & resNames(idx) & """ "
            
            If dictElementUsage.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then
                Dim usageList As Object
                Set usageList = dictElementUsage(resNames(idx))
                Dim entry As Variant
                For Each entry In usageList
                    usageStr = usageStr & entry & " "
                Next entry
            End If
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 4).Value = Trim(usageStr)
            
            rowIdx = rowIdx + 1
        Next idx
        
        With wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & (rowIdx - 1))
            .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
            .Columns.AutoFit
        End With
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & startRowRes).Font.Bold = True
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & startRowRes).Interior.Color = RGB(220, 240, 200)
    End If
    
    wsRes.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub
```

### Что добавлено:

1. **Тип `TElementUsage`** - для хранения информации об использовании элемента (имя операции, время начала, время окончания)

2. **Словарь `dictElementUsage`** - для каждого элемента хранит список всех операций, которые его использовали

3. **В `UpdateResources`** - при каждом использовании элемента добавляется запись в формате: `("Операция 1" 9 20)`

4. **В выходной таблице** - добавлен столбец "Список операций (Операция Начало Конец)" с полным списком использования элемента

### Пример результата:
```
Элемент | Сумм. время | Сумм. задержка | Список операций
--------|-------------|----------------|----------------------------------
ВхI     | 25          | 67             | "ВхI" ("Операция 1" 0 5) ("Операция 2" 10 15) ...
1       | 20          | 0              | "1" ("Операция 1" 0 5) ("Операция 3" 15 20) ...
```

### USER
Теперь в Данные\\Элемент предусмотреть вариантные маршруты прохождения заявки, разделяем точкой с запятой (;). Если заявка идет с задержкой, то  определили время начала обслуживания заявки по первому варианту, потом по второму, если по второму варианту обслуживание начинается раньше, то задействуем его. 

### ASSISTANT
Понял! Добавляю поддержку вариантных маршрутов. Заявка может иметь несколько альтернативных наборов элементов (разделены `;`), и выбирается тот вариант, который позволяет начать обслуживание раньше.

Вот полный обновленный код:

```vba
Option Explicit

Const COL_NAME As Integer = 1
Const COL_ARRIVAL As Integer = 2
Const COL_ELEMENTS As Integer = 3
Const COL_PRIORITY As Integer = 4

Const SHEET_DATA As String = "Данные"
Const SHEET_RESULT As String = "Результаты"

Type TElement
    Name As String
    DurationNoDelay As Double
    DurationWithDelay As Double
End Type

Type TRouteVariant
    Elements() As TElement
    ElementCount As Long
    ChannelName As String
End Type

Type TElementUsage
    OperationName As String
    StartTime As Double
    EndTime As Double
End Type

Type TRequest
    ID As Long
    Name As String
    OriginalElements As String
    Variants() As TRouteVariant
    VariantCount As Long
    SelectedVariant As Long          ' Индекс выбранного варианта (0-based)
    Priority As Double
    ArrivalTime As Double
    StartTime As Double
    EndTime As Double
    ActualDuration As Double
    DelayCausedBy As String
    Status As String
End Type

Sub RunSimulation()
    Dim wsData As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsData = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_DATA)
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If wsData Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "Лист '" & SHEET_DATA & "' не найден!", vbCritical
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = wsData.Cells(wsData.Rows.Count, COL_NAME).End(xlUp).Row
    
    If lastRow < 2 Then
        MsgBox "Нет данных для моделирования (начните со 2-й строки).", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim requests() As TRequest
    ReDim requests(1 To lastRow - 1)
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        requests(i - 1).ID = i - 1
        requests(i - 1).Name = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_NAME).Value)
        
        Dim elementsStr As String
        elementsStr = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ELEMENTS).Value)
        requests(i - 1).OriginalElements = elementsStr
        
        ' Парсинг вариантных маршрутов
        ParseRouteVariants elementsStr, requests(i - 1)
        
        requests(i - 1).Priority = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_PRIORITY).Value)
        
        If IsNumeric(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) And Not IsEmpty(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) Then
            requests(i - 1).ArrivalTime = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value)
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка в строке " & i & ": отсутствует или некорректно указано 'Время поступления' (Столбец B).", vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).ActualDuration = 0
        requests(i - 1).DelayCausedBy = ""
        requests(i - 1).StartTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).EndTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).SelectedVariant = -1
        requests(i - 1).Status = "New"
    Next i

    Dim currentTime As Double
    currentTime = 0
    
    Dim dictResources As Object
    Set dictResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResTotalTime As Object
    Set dictResTotalTime = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResDelayCaused As Object
    Set dictResDelayCaused = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    Dim dictElementUsage As Object
    Set dictElementUsage = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")

    Dim allCompleted As Boolean
    Dim processed As Boolean
    Dim nextTime As Double
    Dim req As Long
    
    Do
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime <= currentTime Then
                requests(req).Status = "Waiting"
            End If
        Next req

        allCompleted = True
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status <> "Completed" Then
                allCompleted = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next req
        If allCompleted Then Exit Do

        processed = False
        
        Dim waitingCount As Long
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then waitingCount = waitingCount + 1
        Next req

        If waitingCount > 0 Then
            Dim dictChannelSums As Object
            Set dictChannelSums = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            
            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    ' Используем канал из ПЕРВОГО варианта как идентификатор канала
                    Dim ch As String
                    If requests(req).VariantCount > 0 Then
                        ch = Trim(CStr(requests(req).Variants(0).ChannelName))
                    Else
                        ch = "Без_канала"
                    End If
                    
                    If ch = "" Then ch = "Без_канала"
                    
                    If Not dictChannelSums.Exists(ch) Then
                        dictChannelSums.Add ch, 0#
                    End If
                    dictChannelSums(ch) = dictChannelSums(ch) + (currentTime - (requests(req).ArrivalTime - requests(req).Priority))
                End If
            Next req

            If dictChannelSums.Count > 0 Then
                Dim channels() As Variant
                ReDim channels(1 To dictChannelSums.Count)
                Dim k As Long, key As Variant
                k = 1
                For Each key In dictChannelSums.Keys
                    channels(k) = key
                    k = k + 1
                Next key

                Dim j As Long, tempCh As Variant
                For i = 1 To UBound(channels) - 1
                    For j = i + 1 To UBound(channels)
                        If dictChannelSums(channels(j)) > dictChannelSums(channels(i)) Then
                            tempCh = channels(i)
                            channels(i) = channels(j)
                            channels(j) = tempCh
                        End If
                    Next j
                Next i

                For i = 1 To UBound(channels)
                    Dim bestCh As String
                    bestCh = channels(i)

                    Dim headReqIdx As Long
                    headReqIdx = -1
                    Dim minArr As Double
                    minArr = 999999999#

                    For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                        If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                            Dim reqChannel As String
                            If requests(req).VariantCount > 0 Then
                                reqChannel = Trim(CStr(requests(req).Variants(0).ChannelName))
                            Else
                                reqChannel = "Без_канала"
                            End If
                            
                            If reqChannel = bestCh Then
                                If requests(req).ArrivalTime < minArr Then
                                    minArr = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                                    headReqIdx = req
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next req

                    If headReqIdx <> -1 Then
                        ' === ВЫБОР ЛУЧШЕГО ВАРИАНТА МАРШРУТА ===
                        Dim bestVariant As Long
                        Dim bestStartTime As Double
                        bestVariant = -1
                        bestStartTime = 999999999#
                        
                        Dim v As Long
                        For v = 0 To requests(headReqIdx).VariantCount - 1
                            Dim variantStartTime As Double
                            variantStartTime = GetVariantStartTime(requests(headReqIdx), v, currentTime, dictResources)
                            
                            If variantStartTime < bestStartTime Then
                                bestStartTime = variantStartTime
                                bestVariant = v
                            End If
                        Next v
                        
                        If bestVariant >= 0 Then
                            requests(headReqIdx).StartTime = bestStartTime
                            requests(headReqIdx).SelectedVariant = bestVariant
                            
                            Dim isDelayed As Boolean
                            isDelayed = (requests(headReqIdx).StartTime > requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
                            
                            Dim channelDuration As Double
                            If isDelayed Then
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVariant).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
                            Else
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVariant).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
                            End If
                            
                            requests(headReqIdx).ActualDuration = channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).EndTime = bestStartTime + channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).Status = "Completed"
                            
                            If isDelayed Then
                                Dim bottleneckRes As String
                                bottleneckRes = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVariant).ChannelName
                                
                                Dim delayAmount As Double
                                delayAmount = requests(headReqIdx).StartTime - requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime
                                
                                If Not dictResDelayCaused.Exists(bottleneckRes) Then
                                    dictResDelayCaused.Add bottleneckRes, 0#
                                End If
                                dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) = dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) + delayAmount
                                
                                requests(headReqIdx).DelayCausedBy = bottleneckRes
                            End If
                            
                            UpdateResources requests(headReqIdx), dictResources, dictResTotalTime, dictElementUsage
                            
                            processed = True
                            Exit For 
                        End If
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
        End If

        If Not processed Then
            nextTime = 999999999#

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime > currentTime Then
                    If requests(req).ArrivalTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim relTime As Double
                    relTime = 999999999#
                    
                    ' Ищем ближайшее время освобождения для ЛЮБОГО варианта
                    For v = 0 To requests(req).VariantCount - 1
                        Dim variantRelTime As Double
                        variantRelTime = GetEarliestVariantRelease(requests(req), v, currentTime, dictResources)
                        If variantRelTime < relTime Then
                            relTime = variantRelTime
                        End If
                    Next v
                    
                    If relTime > currentTime And relTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = relTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            If nextTime = 999999999# Then Exit Do
            
            currentTime = nextTime
        End If
    Loop

    OutputResults requests, dictResTotalTime, dictResDelayCaused, dictElementUsage
    
    MsgBox "Моделирование завершено успешно!", vbInformation
End Sub

' Парсинг вариантных маршрутов
Sub ParseRouteVariants(elementsStr As String, ByRef req As TRequest)
    Dim variantsArr() As String
    variantsArr = Split(elementsStr, ";")
    
    Dim variantCount As Long
    variantCount = UBound(variantsArr) - LBound(variantsArr) + 1
    
    ReDim req.Variants(0 To variantCount - 1)
    req.VariantCount = variantCount
    
    Dim v As Long
    For v = LBound(variantsArr) To UBound(variantsArr)
        Dim variantStr As String
        variantStr = Trim(variantsArr(v))
        
        ParseVariant variantStr, req.Variants(v)
    Next v
```
