End Sub

' Парсинг одного варианта (список элементов через запятую)
Sub ParseVariant(variantStr As String, ByRef variant As TRouteVariant)
    Dim arr() As String
    arr = Split(variantStr, ",")
    
    Dim count As Long
    count = UBound(arr) - LBound(arr) + 1
    
    ReDim variant.Elements(0 To count - 1)
    variant.ElementCount = count
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr)
        Dim part As String
        part = Trim(arr(i))
        
        Dim parts() As String
        parts = Split(part, ":")
        
        If UBound(parts) >= 2 Then
            variant.Elements(i).Name = Trim(parts(0))
            variant.Elements(i).DurationNoDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(1)))
            variant.Elements(i).DurationWithDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(2)))
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка парсинга элемента: " & part, vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next i
    
    ' Первый элемент - канал
    If count > 0 Then
        variant.ChannelName = variant.Elements(0).Name
    Else
        variant.ChannelName = "Без_канала"
    End If
End Sub

' Определение времени начала обслуживания для конкретного варианта
Function GetVariantStartTime(req As TRequest, variantIdx As Long, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    Dim earliestStart As Double
    earliestStart = currentTime
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(variantIdx).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(variantIdx).Elements(i).Name
        
        If dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim busyUntil As Double
            busyUntil = dictRes(resName)
            If busyUntil > earliestStart Then
                earliestStart = busyUntil
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    GetVariantStartTime = earliestStart
End Function

' Определение ближайшего времени освобождения для варианта
Function GetEarliestVariantRelease(req As TRequest, variantIdx As Long, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    Dim earliest As Double
    earliest = 999999999#
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(variantIdx).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(variantIdx).Elements(i).Name
        
        If dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim busyUntil As Double
            busyUntil = dictRes(resName)
            If busyUntil > currentTime And busyUntil < earliest Then
                earliest = busyUntil
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    GetEarliestVariantRelease = earliest
End Function

Function AreResourcesFree(req As TRequest, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Boolean
    ' Теперь проверяется только выбранный вариант
    If req.SelectedVariant < 0 Then
        AreResourcesFree = False
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    Dim i As Long
    AreResourcesFree = True
    
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            dictRes.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        
        If dictRes(resName) > currentTime Then
            AreResourcesFree = False
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

Sub UpdateResources(req As TRequest, dictRes As Object, dictResTotal As Object, dictElementUsage As Object)
    If req.SelectedVariant < 0 Then Exit Sub
    
    Dim i As Long
    
    Dim duration As Double
    Dim isDelayed As Boolean
    isDelayed = (req.StartTime > req.ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
    
    If isDelayed Then
        duration = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
    Else
        duration = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
    End If
    
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            dictRes.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        
        If req.EndTime > dictRes(resName) Then
            dictRes(resName) = req.EndTime
        End If
        
        If Not dictResTotal.Exists(resName) Then
            dictResTotal.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        dictResTotal(resName) = dictResTotal(resName) + duration
        
        ' Добавление в список использования
        If Not dictElementUsage.Exists(resName) Then
            dictElementUsage.Add resName, CreateObject("System.Collections.ArrayList")
        End If
        
        Dim usageList As Object
        Set usageList = dictElementUsage(resName)
        
        Dim usageEntry As String
        usageEntry = "(""" & req.Name & """ " & req.StartTime & " " & req.EndTime & ")"
        usageList.Add usageEntry
    Next i
End Sub

Function GetEarliestResourceRelease(req As TRequest, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    ' Для совместимости - используем первый вариант
    If req.VariantCount > 0 Then
        GetEarliestResourceRelease = GetEarliestVariantRelease(req, 0, currentTime, dictRes)
    Else
        GetEarliestResourceRelease = 999999999#
    End If
End Function

Sub OutputResults(requests() As TRequest, dictResTotal As Object, dictResDelay As Object, dictElementUsage As Object)
    Dim wsRes As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_RESULT)
    If wsRes Is Nothing Then
        Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        wsRes.Name = SHEET_RESULT
    Else
        wsRes.Cells.Clear
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0

    wsRes.Cells(1, 1).Value = "Наименование"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 2).Value = "Элементы (варианты)"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 3).Value = "Выбранный вариант"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 4).Value = "Время поступл."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 5).Value = "Начало обслуж."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 6).Value = "Время окончания"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 7).Value = "Факт. длительность"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 8).Value = "Задержка"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 9).Value = "Элементы, вызвавшие задержку"
    
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To UBound(requests)
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 1).Value = requests(r).Name
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 2).Value = requests(r).OriginalElements
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 3).Value = requests(r).SelectedVariant + 1  ' +1 для отображения (1-based)
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 4).Value = requests(r).ArrivalTime
        
        If requests(r).StartTime >= 0 Then
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 5).Value = requests(r).StartTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 6).Value = requests(r).EndTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 7).Value = requests(r).ActualDuration
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 8).Value = requests(r).StartTime - requests(r).ArrivalTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 9).Value = requests(r).DelayCausedBy
        Else
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 5).Value = "Ошибка"
        End If
    Next r
    
    With wsRes.Range("A1:I" & UBound(requests) + 1)
        .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Columns.AutoFit
    End With
    wsRes.Range("A1:I1").Font.Bold = True
    wsRes.Range("A1:I1").Interior.Color = RGB(200, 220, 240)

    ' Таблица статистики по элементам
    Dim startRowRes As Long
    startRowRes = UBound(requests) + 4
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 1).Value = "Элемент"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 2).Value = "Суммарное время занятости"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 3).Value = "Суммарная задержка, вызванная элементом"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 4).Value = "Список операций"
    
    Dim allResources As Object
    Set allResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In dictResTotal.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key
    For Each key In dictResDelay.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key

    Dim resCount As Long
    resCount = allResources.Count
    
    If resCount > 0 Then
        Dim resNames() As String
        Dim resDelays() As Double
        ReDim resNames(1 To resCount)
        ReDim resDelays(1 To resCount)
        
        Dim idx As Long
        idx = 1
        For Each key In allResources.Keys
            resNames(idx) = key
            If dictResDelay.Exists(key) Then
                resDelays(idx) = dictResDelay(key)
            Else
                resDelays(idx) = 0
            End If
            idx = idx + 1
        Next key
        
        Dim m As Long, n As Long
        Dim tempName As String, tempDelay As Double
        For m = 1 To resCount - 1
            For n = m + 1 To resCount
                If resDelays(n) > resDelays(m) Then
                    tempDelay = resDelays(m)
                    resDelays(m) = resDelays(n)
                    resDelays(n) = tempDelay
                    
                    tempName = resNames(m)
                    resNames(m) = resNames(n)
                    resNames(n) = tempName
                End If
            Next n
        Next m
        
        Dim rowIdx As Long
        rowIdx = startRowRes + 1
        For idx = 1 To resCount
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 1).Value = resNames(idx)
            
            Dim totalBusy As Double
            totalBusy = 0
            If dictResTotal.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then totalBusy = dictResTotal(resNames(idx))
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 2).Value = totalBusy
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 3).Value = resDelays(idx)
            
            Dim usageStr As String
            usageStr = """" & resNames(idx) & """ "
            
            If dictElementUsage.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then
                Dim usageList As Object
                Set usageList = dictElementUsage(resNames(idx))
                Dim entry As Variant
                For Each entry In usageList
                    usageStr = usageStr & entry & " "
                Next entry
            End If
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 4).Value = Trim(usageStr)
            
            rowIdx = rowIdx + 1
        Next idx
        
        With wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & (rowIdx - 1))
            .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
            .Columns.AutoFit
        End With
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & startRowRes).Font.Bold = True
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & startRowRes).Interior.Color = RGB(220, 240, 200)
    End If
    
    wsRes.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub
```

### Что добавлено:

1. **Тип `TRouteVariant`** - описывает один вариант маршрута (набор элементов)

2. **Парсинг**: 
   - Сначала строка делится по `;` на варианты
   - Каждый вариант делится по `,` на элементы
   - Пример: `ВхI:5:10,1:5:10;ВхII:5:10,2:5:10` → 2 варианта

3. **Выбор лучшего варианта**:
   - Для каждой заявки проверяются ВСЕ варианты
   - Для каждого варианта вычисляется время начала (когда освободятся ВСЕ элементы варианта)
   - Выбирается вариант с **наименьшим** временем начала

4. **В выходных данных**:
   - Добавлен столбец "Выбранный вариант" (номер варианта 1, 2, 3...)
   - Столбец "Элементы (варианты)" показывает все варианты через `;`

### Пример данных:
```
Операция1 | 0 | ВхI:5:10,1:5:10;ВхII:5:10,2:5:10 | 2
```

Если ВхI занят до 10, а ВхII свободен с 0, то:
- Вариант 1 начнется в 10
- Вариант 2 начнется в 0
- **Выберется вариант 2** (ВхII,2)

### USER
Отдельная таблица на листе исходных данных где для входных элементов указать количество каналов (может быть не один). Нужно отслеживать количество занятых каналов и протоколе для таких элементов указывать, какой именно канал занят

### ASSISTANT
Отлично! Добавляю поддержку нескольких каналов для одного элемента. Теперь каждый элемент может иметь несколько идентичных каналов, и модель будет отслеживать, какой именно канал занят.

Вот полный обновленный код:

```vba
Option Explicit

Const COL_NAME As Integer = 1
Const COL_ARRIVAL As Integer = 2
Const COL_ELEMENTS As Integer = 3
Const COL_PRIORITY As Integer = 4

Const SHEET_DATA As String = "Данные"
Const SHEET_RESULT As String = "Результаты"
Const SHEET_CHANNELS As String = "Каналы"

Const COL_CH_NAME As Integer = 1
Const COL_CH_COUNT As Integer = 2

Type TElement
    Name As String
    DurationNoDelay As Double
    DurationWithDelay As Double
End Type

Type TRouteVariant
    Elements() As TElement
    ElementCount As Long
    ChannelName As String
End Type

Type TRequest
    ID As Long
    Name As String
    OriginalElements As String
    Variants() As TRouteVariant
    VariantCount As Long
    SelectedVariant As Long
    Priority As Double
    ArrivalTime As Double
    StartTime As Double
    EndTime As Double
    ActualDuration As Double
    DelayCausedBy As String
    SelectedChannel As String        ' Например: "ВхI(канал 2)"
    Status As String
End Type

Sub RunSimulation()
    Dim wsData As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsData = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_DATA)
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If wsData Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "Лист '" & SHEET_DATA & "' не найден!", vbCritical
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' Чтение таблицы каналов
    Dim dictChannelCounts As Object
    Set dictChannelCounts = ReadChannelCounts()

    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = wsData.Cells(wsData.Rows.Count, COL_NAME).End(xlUp).Row
    
    If lastRow < 2 Then
        MsgBox "Нет данных для моделирования (начните со 2-й строки).", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim requests() As TRequest
    ReDim requests(1 To lastRow - 1)
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        requests(i - 1).ID = i - 1
        requests(i - 1).Name = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_NAME).Value)
        
        Dim elementsStr As String
        elementsStr = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ELEMENTS).Value)
        requests(i - 1).OriginalElements = elementsStr
        
        ParseRouteVariants elementsStr, requests(i - 1)
        
        requests(i - 1).Priority = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_PRIORITY).Value)
        
        If IsNumeric(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) And Not IsEmpty(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) Then
            requests(i - 1).ArrivalTime = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value)
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка в строке " & i & ": отсутствует или некорректно указано 'Время поступления' (Столбец B).", vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).ActualDuration = 0
        requests(i - 1).DelayCausedBy = ""
        requests(i - 1).SelectedChannel = ""
        requests(i - 1).StartTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).EndTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).SelectedVariant = -1
        requests(i - 1).Status = "New"
    Next i

    Dim currentTime As Double
    currentTime = 0
    
    ' dictResources теперь хранит массив времени освобождения каждого канала
    ' Например: dictResources("ВхI") = Array(10, 15, 0) - 3 канала, первый занят до 10, второй до 15, третий свободен
    Dim dictResources As Object
    Set dictResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    ' Инициализация ресурсов
    InitResources dictResources, dictChannelCounts

    Dim dictResTotalTime As Object
    Set dictResTotalTime = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResDelayCaused As Object
    Set dictResDelayCaused = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    Dim dictElementUsage As Object
    Set dictElementUsage = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")

    Dim allCompleted As Boolean
    Dim processed As Boolean
    Dim nextTime As Double
    Dim req As Long
    
    Do
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime <= currentTime Then
                requests(req).Status = "Waiting"
            End If
        Next req

        allCompleted = True
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status <> "Completed" Then
                allCompleted = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next req
        If allCompleted Then Exit Do

        processed = False
        
        Dim waitingCount As Long
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then waitingCount = waitingCount + 1
        Next req

        If waitingCount > 0 Then
            Dim dictChannelSums As Object
            Set dictChannelSums = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            
            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim ch As String
                    If requests(req).VariantCount > 0 Then
                        ch = Trim(CStr(requests(req).Variants(0).ChannelName))
                    Else
                        ch = "Без_канала"
                    End If
                    
                    If ch = "" Then ch = "Без_канала"
                    
                    If Not dictChannelSums.Exists(ch) Then
                        dictChannelSums.Add ch, 0#
                    End If
                    dictChannelSums(ch) = dictChannelSums(ch) + (currentTime - (requests(req).ArrivalTime - requests(req).Priority))
                End If
            Next req

            If dictChannelSums.Count > 0 Then
                Dim channels() As Variant
                ReDim channels(1 To dictChannelSums.Count)
                Dim k As Long, key As Variant
                k = 1
                For Each key In dictChannelSums.Keys
                    channels(k) = key
                    k = k + 1
                Next key

                Dim j As Long, tempCh As Variant
                For i = 1 To UBound(channels) - 1
                    For j = i + 1 To UBound(channels)
                        If dictChannelSums(channels(j)) > dictChannelSums(channels(i)) Then
                            tempCh = channels(i)
                            channels(i) = channels(j)
                            channels(j) = tempCh
                        End If
                    Next j
                Next i

                For i = 1 To UBound(channels)
                    Dim bestCh As String
                    bestCh = channels(i)

                    Dim headReqIdx As Long
                    headReqIdx = -1
                    Dim minArr As Double
                    minArr = 999999999#

                    For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                        If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                            Dim reqChannel As String
                            If requests(req).VariantCount > 0 Then
                                reqChannel = Trim(CStr(requests(req).Variants(0).ChannelName))
                            Else
                                reqChannel = "Без_канала"
                            End If
                            
                            If reqChannel = bestCh Then
                                If requests(req).ArrivalTime < minArr Then
                                    minArr = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                                    headReqIdx = req
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next req

                    If headReqIdx <> -1 Then
                        ' Выбор лучшего варианта маршрута
                        Dim bestVariant As Long
                        Dim bestStartTime As Double
                        bestVariant = -1
                        bestStartTime = 999999999#
                        
                        Dim v As Long
                        For v = 0 To requests(headReqIdx).VariantCount - 1
                            Dim variantStartTime As Double
                            variantStartTime = GetVariantStartTime(requests(headReqIdx), v, currentTime, dictResources)
                            
                            If variantStartTime < bestStartTime Then
                                bestStartTime = variantStartTime
                                bestVariant = v
                            End If
                        Next v
                        
                        If bestVariant >= 0 Then
                            requests(headReqIdx).StartTime = bestStartTime
                            requests(headReqIdx).SelectedVariant = bestVariant
                            
                            Dim isDelayed As Boolean
                            isDelayed = (requests(headReqIdx).StartTime > requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
                            
                            Dim channelDuration As Double
                            If isDelayed Then
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVariant).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
                            Else
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVariant).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
                            End If
                            
                            requests(headReqIdx).ActualDuration = channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).EndTime = bestStartTime + channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).Status = "Completed"
                            
                            If isDelayed Then
                                Dim bottleneckRes As String
                                bottleneckRes = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVariant).ChannelName
                                
                                Dim delayAmount As Double
                                delayAmount = requests(headReqIdx).StartTime - requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime
                                
                                If Not dictResDelayCaused.Exists(bottleneckRes) Then
                                    dictResDelayCaused.Add bottleneckRes, 0#
                                End If
                                dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) = dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) + delayAmount
                                
                                requests(headReqIdx).DelayCausedBy = bottleneckRes
                            End If
                            
                            UpdateResources requests(headReqIdx), dictResources, dictResTotalTime, dictElementUsage, dictChannelCounts
                            
                            processed = True
                            Exit For 
                        End If
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
        End If

        If Not processed Then
            nextTime = 999999999#

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime > currentTime Then
                    If requests(req).ArrivalTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim relTime As Double
                    relTime = 999999999#
                    
                    For v = 0 To requests(req).VariantCount - 1
                        Dim variantRelTime As Double
                        variantRelTime = GetEarliestVariantRelease(requests(req), v, currentTime, dictResources)
                        If variantRelTime < relTime Then
                            relTime = variantRelTime
                        End If
                    Next v
                    
                    If relTime > currentTime And relTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = relTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            If nextTime = 999999999# Then Exit Do
            
            currentTime = nextTime
        End If
    Loop

    OutputResults requests, dictResTotalTime, dictResDelayCaused, dictElementUsage
    
    MsgBox "Моделирование завершено успешно!", vbInformation
End Sub

' Чтение таблицы каналов с листа "Каналы"
Function ReadChannelCounts() As Object
    Dim dict As Object
    Set dict = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_CHANNELS)
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        ' Если листа нет, создаем с примером
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        ws.Name = SHEET_CHANNELS
        ws.Cells(1, 1).Value = "Элемент"
        ws.Cells(1, 2).Value = "Количество каналов"
        ws.Cells(2, 1).Value = "ВхI"
        ws.Cells(2, 2).Value = 2
        MsgBox "Создан лист 'Каналы'. Укажите количество каналов для каждого элемента.", vbInformation
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, COL_CH_NAME).End(xlUp).Row
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        Dim elemName As String
        elemName = Trim(CStr(ws.Cells(i, COL_CH_NAME).Value))
        If elemName <> "" Then
            Dim chCount As Long
            If IsNumeric(ws.Cells(i, COL_CH_COUNT).Value) Then
                chCount = CLng(ws.Cells(i, COL_CH_COUNT).Value)
            Else
                chCount = 1
            End If
            If chCount < 1 Then chCount = 1
            dict.Add elemName, chCount
        End If
    Next i
    
    Set ReadChannelCounts = dict
End Function

' Инициализация ресурсов с учетом количества каналов
Sub InitResources(dictRes As Object, dictChannelCounts As Object)
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In dictChannelCounts.Keys
        Dim chCount As Long
        chCount = dictChannelCounts(key)
        
        Dim times() As Double
        ReDim times(0 To chCount - 1)
        Dim i As Long
        For i = 0 To chCount - 1
            times(i) = 0#
        Next i
        
        dictRes.Add key, times
    Next key
End Sub

' Парсинг вариантных маршрутов
Sub ParseRouteVariants(elementsStr As String, ByRef req As TRequest)
    Dim variantsArr() As String
    variantsArr = Split(elementsStr, ";")
    
    Dim variantCount As Long
    variantCount = UBound(variantsArr) - LBound(variantsArr) + 1
    
    ReDim req.Variants(0 To variantCount - 1)
    req.VariantCount = variantCount
    
    Dim v As Long
    For v = LBound(variantsArr) To UBound(variantsArr)
        Dim variantStr As String
        variantStr = Trim(variantsArr(v))
        
        ParseVariant variantStr, req.Variants(v)
    Next v
End Sub

Sub ParseVariant(variantStr As String, ByRef variant As TRouteVariant)
    Dim arr() As String
    arr = Split(variantStr, ",")
    
    Dim count As Long
    count = UBound(arr) - LBound(arr) + 1
    
    ReDim variant.Elements(0 To count - 1)
    variant.ElementCount = count
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr)
        Dim part As String
        part = Trim(arr(i))
        
        Dim parts() As String
        parts = Split(part, ":")
        
        If UBound(parts) >= 2 Then
            variant.Elements(i).Name = Trim(parts(0))
            variant.Elements(i).DurationNoDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(1)))
            variant.Elements(i).DurationWithDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(2)))
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка парсинга элемента: " & part, vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next i
    
    If count > 0 Then
        variant.ChannelName = variant.Elements(0).Name
    Else
        variant.ChannelName = "Без_канала"
    End If
End Sub

' Определение времени начала для варианта с учетом нескольких каналов
Function GetVariantStartTime(req As TRequest, variantIdx As Long, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    Dim earliestStart As Double
    earliestStart = currentTime
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(variantIdx).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(variantIdx).Elements(i).Name
        
        If dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim times() As Double
            times = dictRes(resName)
            
            ' Находим канал, который освободится раньше всего
            Dim minFreeTime As Double
            minFreeTime = 999999999#
            Dim j As Long
            For j = 0 To UBound(times)
                If times(j) < minFreeTime Then
                    minFreeTime = times(j)
                End If
            Next j
            
            ' Если все каналы заняты после currentTime, берем минимальное время
            If minFreeTime > earliestStart Then
                earliestStart = minFreeTime
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    GetVariantStartTime = earliestStart
End Function

Function GetEarliestVariantRelease(req As TRequest, variantIdx As Long, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    Dim earliest As Double
    earliest = 999999999#
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(variantIdx).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(variantIdx).Elements(i).Name
        
        If dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim times() As Double
            times = dictRes(resName)
            
            Dim j As Long
            For j = 0 To UBound(times)
                If times(j) > currentTime And times(j) < earliest Then
                    earliest = times(j)
                End If
            Next j
        End If
    Next i
    
    GetEarliestVariantRelease = earliest
End Function

Sub UpdateResources(req As TRequest, dictRes As Object, dictResTotal As Object, dictElementUsage As Object, dictChannelCounts As Object)
    If req.SelectedVariant < 0 Then Exit Sub
    
    Dim i As Long
    
    Dim duration As Double
    Dim isDelayed As Boolean
    isDelayed = (req.StartTime > req.ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
    
    If isDelayed Then
        duration = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
    Else
        duration = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
    End If
    
    Dim channelInfo As String
    channelInfo = ""
    
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            ' Если ресурс не инициализирован, создаем с 1 каналом
            Dim times(0) As Double
            times(0) = 0#
            dictRes.Add resName, times
        End If
        
        Dim times() As Double
        times = dictRes(resName)
        
        ' Находим канал с минимальным временем освобождения
        Dim bestChannel As Long
        bestChannel = 0
        Dim minTime As Double
        minTime = times(0)
        
        Dim j As Long
        For j = 1 To UBound(times)
            If times(j) < minTime Then
                minTime = times(j)
                bestChannel = j
            End If
        Next j
        
        ' Занимаем этот канал
        times(bestChannel) = req.EndTime
        dictRes(resName) = times
        
        ' Формируем информацию о канале
        If channelInfo = "" Then
            channelInfo = resName & "(канал " & (bestChannel + 1) & ")"
        Else
            channelInfo = channelInfo & ", " & resName & "(канал " & (bestChannel + 1) & ")"
        End If
        
        ' Статистика
        If Not dictResTotal.Exists(resName) Then
            dictResTotal.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        dictResTotal(resName) = dictResTotal(resName) + duration
        
        ' Список использования
        If Not dictElementUsage.Exists(resName) Then
            dictElementUsage.Add resName, CreateObject("System.Collections.ArrayList")
        End If
        
        Dim usageList As Object
        Set usageList = dictElementUsage(resName)
        
        Dim usageEntry As String
        usageEntry = "(""" & req.Name & """ " & req.StartTime & " " & req.EndTime & " к" & (bestChannel + 1) & ")"
        usageList.Add usageEntry
    Next i
    
    req.SelectedChannel = channelInfo
End Sub

Sub OutputResults(requests() As TRequest, dictResTotal As Object, dictResDelay As Object, dictElementUsage As Object)
    Dim wsRes As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_RESULT)
    If wsRes Is Nothing Then
        Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        wsRes.Name = SHEET_RESULT
    Else
        wsRes.Cells.Clear
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0

    wsRes.Cells(1, 1).Value = "Наименование"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 2).Value = "Элементы (варианты)"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 3).Value = "Выбранный вариант"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 4).Value = "Занятые каналы"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 5).Value = "Время поступл."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 6).Value = "Начало обслуж."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 7).Value = "Время окончания"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 8).Value = "Факт. длительность"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 9).Value = "Задержка"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 10).Value = "Элементы, вызвавшие задержку"
    
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To UBound(requests)
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 1).Value = requests(r).Name
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 2).Value = requests(r).OriginalElements
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 3).Value = requests(r).SelectedVariant + 1
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 4).Value = requests(r).SelectedChannel
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 5).Value = requests(r).ArrivalTime
        
        If requests(r).StartTime >= 0 Then
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 6).Value = requests(r).StartTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 7).Value = requests(r).EndTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 8).Value = requests(r).ActualDuration
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 9).Value = requests(r).StartTime - requests(r).ArrivalTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 10).Value = requests(r).DelayCausedBy
        Else
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 6).Value = "Ошибка"
        End If
    Next r
    
    With wsRes.Range("A1:J" & UBound(requests) + 1)
        .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Columns.AutoFit
    End With
    wsRes.Range("A1:J1").Font.Bold = True
    wsRes.Range("A1:J1").Interior.Color = RGB(200, 220, 240)

    ' Таблица статистики
    Dim startRowRes As Long
    startRowRes = UBound(requests) + 4
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 1).Value = "Элемент"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 2).Value = "Суммарное время занятости"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 3).Value = "Суммарная задержка, вызванная элементом"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 4).Value = "Список операций"
    
    Dim allResources As Object
    Set allResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In dictResTotal.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key
    For Each key In dictResDelay.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key

    Dim resCount As Long
    resCount = allResources.Count
    
    If resCount > 0 Then
        Dim resNames() As String
        Dim resDelays() As Double
        ReDim resNames(1 To resCount)
        ReDim resDelays(1 To resCount)
        
        Dim idx As Long
        idx = 1
        For Each key In allResources.Keys
            resNames(idx) = key
            If dictResDelay.Exists(key) Then
                resDelays(idx) = dictResDelay(key)
            Else
                resDelays(idx) = 0
            End If
            idx = idx + 1
        Next key
        
        Dim m As Long, n As Long
        Dim tempName As String, tempDelay As Double
        For m = 1 To resCount - 1
            For n = m + 1 To resCount
                If resDelays(n) > resDelays(m) Then
                    tempDelay = resDelays(m)
                    resDelays(m) = resDelays(n)
                    resDelays(n) = tempDelay
                    
                    tempName = resNames(m)
                    resNames(m) = resNames(n)
                    resNames(n) = tempName
                End If
            Next n
        Next m
        
        Dim rowIdx As Long
        rowIdx = startRowRes + 1
        For idx = 1 To resCount
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 1).Value = resNames(idx)
            
            Dim totalBusy As Double
            totalBusy = 0
            If dictResTotal.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then totalBusy = dictResTotal(resNames(idx))
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 2).Value = totalBusy
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 3).Value = resDelays(idx)
            
            Dim usageStr As String
            usageStr = """" & resNames(idx) & """ "
            
            If dictElementUsage.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then
                Dim usageList As Object
                Set usageList = dictElementUsage(resNames(idx))
                Dim entry As Variant
                For Each entry In usageList
                    usageStr = usageStr & entry & " "
                Next entry
            End If
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 4).Value = Trim(usageStr)
            
            rowIdx = rowIdx + 1
        Next idx
        
        With wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & (rowIdx - 1))
            .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
            .Columns.AutoFit
        End With
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & startRowRes).Font.Bold = True
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & startRowRes).Interior.Color = RGB(220, 240, 200)
    End If
    
    wsRes.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub
```

### Что изменилось:

1. **Новый лист "Каналы"** с таблицей:
   | Элемент | Количество каналов |
   |---------|-------------------|
   | ВхI     | 2                 |
   | ВхII    | 1                 |
   | 1       | 3                 |

2. **Массив времени освобождения**: каждый элемент теперь хранит массив `times()`, где `times(i)` - время освобождения i-го канала.

3. **Выбор канала**: при занятии ресурса выбирается канал с минимальным временем освобождения.

4. **В протоколе**: добавлен столбец "Занятые каналы" с форматом `ВхI(канал 1), 1(канал 2)`.

5. **В списке операций**: добавлен номер канала `("Операция 1" 0 5 к1)` - к1 означает канал 1.

Если листа "Каналы" нет, он создастся автоматически с примером. Если элемента нет в таблице - считается 1 канал.

### USER


### ASSISTANT
Ошибка "Sub or Function not defined" возникает потому, что VBA не видит процедуру `ParseVariant`. Это может быть из-за того, что:

1. **Типы данных должны быть объявлены в начале модуля** (перед всеми процедурами)
2. Возможно, код разорван или процедуры находятся в разных местах

Вот **полный исправленный код** - скопируйте его целиком и замените всё содержимое модуля:

```vba
Option Explicit

' === ТИПЫ ДАННЫХ (должны быть в начале модуля) ===
Type TElement
    Name As String
    DurationNoDelay As Double
    DurationWithDelay As Double
End Type

Type TRouteVariant
    Elements() As TElement
    ElementCount As Long
    ChannelName As String
End Type

Type TRequest
    ID As Long
    Name As String
    OriginalElements As String
    Variants() As TRouteVariant
    VariantCount As Long
    SelectedVariant As Long
    Priority As Double
    ArrivalTime As Double
    StartTime As Double
    EndTime As Double
    ActualDuration As Double
    DelayCausedBy As String
    SelectedChannel As String
    Status As String
End Type

' === КОНСТАНТЫ ===
Const COL_NAME As Integer = 1
Const COL_ARRIVAL As Integer = 2
Const COL_ELEMENTS As Integer = 3
Const COL_PRIORITY As Integer = 4

Const SHEET_DATA As String = "Данные"
Const SHEET_RESULT As String = "Результаты"
Const SHEET_CHANNELS As String = "Каналы"

Const COL_CH_NAME As Integer = 1
Const COL_CH_COUNT As Integer = 2

' === ОСНОВНАЯ ПРОЦЕДУРА ===
Sub RunSimulation()
    Dim wsData As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsData = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_DATA)
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If wsData Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "Лист '" & SHEET_DATA & "' не найден!", vbCritical
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim dictChannelCounts As Object
    Set dictChannelCounts = ReadChannelCounts()

    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = wsData.Cells(wsData.Rows.Count, COL_NAME).End(xlUp).Row
    
    If lastRow < 2 Then
        MsgBox "Нет данных для моделирования.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim requests() As TRequest
    ReDim requests(1 To lastRow - 1)
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        requests(i - 1).ID = i - 1
        requests(i - 1).Name = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_NAME).Value)
        
        Dim elementsStr As String
        elementsStr = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ELEMENTS).Value)
        requests(i - 1).OriginalElements = elementsStr
        
        ParseRouteVariants elementsStr, requests(i - 1)
        
        requests(i - 1).Priority = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_PRIORITY).Value)
        
        If IsNumeric(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) And Not IsEmpty(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value) Then
            requests(i - 1).ArrivalTime = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value)
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка в строке " & i, vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).ActualDuration = 0
        requests(i - 1).DelayCausedBy = ""
        requests(i - 1).SelectedChannel = ""
        requests(i - 1).StartTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).EndTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).SelectedVariant = -1
        requests(i - 1).Status = "New"
    Next i

    Dim currentTime As Double
    currentTime = 0
    
    Dim dictResources As Object
    Set dictResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    InitResources dictResources, dictChannelCounts

    Dim dictResTotalTime As Object
    Set dictResTotalTime = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResDelayCaused As Object
    Set dictResDelayCaused = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    Dim dictElementUsage As Object
    Set dictElementUsage = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")

    Dim allCompleted As Boolean
    Dim processed As Boolean
    Dim nextTime As Double
    Dim req As Long
    
    Do
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime <= currentTime Then
                requests(req).Status = "Waiting"
            End If
        Next req

        allCompleted = True
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status <> "Completed" Then
                allCompleted = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next req
        If allCompleted Then Exit Do

        processed = False
        
        Dim waitingCount As Long
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then waitingCount = waitingCount + 1
        Next req

        If waitingCount > 0 Then
            Dim dictChannelSums As Object
            Set dictChannelSums = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            
            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim ch As String
                    If requests(req).VariantCount > 0 Then
                        ch = Trim(CStr(requests(req).Variants(0).ChannelName))
                    Else
                        ch = "Без_канала"
                    End If
                    
                    If ch = "" Then ch = "Без_канала"
                    
                    If Not dictChannelSums.Exists(ch) Then
                        dictChannelSums.Add ch, 0#
                    End If
                    dictChannelSums(ch) = dictChannelSums(ch) + (currentTime - (requests(req).ArrivalTime - requests(req).Priority))
                End If
            Next req

            If dictChannelSums.Count > 0 Then
                Dim channels() As Variant
                ReDim channels(1 To dictChannelSums.Count)
                Dim k As Long, key As Variant
                k = 1
                For Each key In dictChannelSums.Keys
                    channels(k) = key
                    k = k + 1
                Next key

                Dim j As Long, tempCh As Variant
                For i = 1 To UBound(channels) - 1
                    For j = i + 1 To UBound(channels)
                        If dictChannelSums(channels(j)) > dictChannelSums(channels(i)) Then
                            tempCh = channels(i)
                            channels(i) = channels(j)
                            channels(j) = tempCh
                        End If
                    Next j
                Next i

                For i = 1 To UBound(channels)
                    Dim bestCh As String
                    bestCh = channels(i)

                    Dim headReqIdx As Long
                    headReqIdx = -1
                    Dim minArr As Double
                    minArr = 999999999#

                    For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                        If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                            Dim reqChannel As String
                            If requests(req).VariantCount > 0 Then
                                reqChannel = Trim(CStr(requests(req).Variants(0).ChannelName))
                            Else
                                reqChannel = "Без_канала"
                            End If
                            
                            If reqChannel = bestCh Then
                                If requests(req).ArrivalTime < minArr Then
                                    minArr = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                                    headReqIdx = req
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next req

                    If headReqIdx <> -1 Then
                        Dim bestVariant As Long
                        Dim bestStartTime As Double
                        bestVariant = -1
                        bestStartTime = 999999999#
                        
                        Dim v As Long
                        For v = 0 To requests(headReqIdx).VariantCount - 1
                            Dim variantStartTime As Double
                            variantStartTime = GetVariantStartTime(requests(headReqIdx), v, currentTime, dictResources)
                            
                            If variantStartTime < bestStartTime Then
                                bestStartTime = variantStartTime
                                bestVariant = v
                            End If
                        Next v
                        
                        If bestVariant >= 0 Then
                            requests(headReqIdx).StartTime = bestStartTime
                            requests(headReqIdx).SelectedVariant = bestVariant
                            
                            Dim isDelayed As Boolean
                            isDelayed = (requests(headReqIdx).StartTime > requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
                            
                            Dim channelDuration As Double
                            If isDelayed Then
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVariant).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
                            Else
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVariant).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
                            End If
                            
                            requests(headReqIdx).ActualDuration = channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).EndTime = bestStartTime + channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).Status = "Completed"
                            
                            If isDelayed Then
                                Dim bottleneckRes As String
                                bottleneckRes = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVariant).ChannelName
                                
                                Dim delayAmount As Double
                                delayAmount = requests(headReqIdx).StartTime - requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime
                                
                                If Not dictResDelayCaused.Exists(bottleneckRes) Then
                                    dictResDelayCaused.Add bottleneckRes, 0#
                                End If
                                dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) = dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) + delayAmount
                                
                                requests(headReqIdx).DelayCausedBy = bottleneckRes
                            End If
                            
                            UpdateResources requests(headReqIdx), dictResources, dictResTotalTime, dictElementUsage, dictChannelCounts
                            
                            processed = True
                            Exit For 
                        End If
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
        End If

        If Not processed Then
            nextTime = 999999999#

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime > currentTime Then
                    If requests(req).ArrivalTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim relTime As Double
                    relTime = 999999999#
                    
                    For v = 0 To requests(req).VariantCount - 1
                        Dim variantRelTime As Double
                        variantRelTime = GetEarliestVariantRelease(requests(req), v, currentTime, dictResources)
                        If variantRelTime < relTime Then
                            relTime = variantRelTime
                        End If
                    Next v
                    
                    If relTime > currentTime And relTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = relTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            If nextTime = 999999999# Then Exit Do
            
            currentTime = nextTime
        End If
    Loop

    OutputResults requests, dictResTotalTime, dictResDelayCaused, dictElementUsage
    
    MsgBox "Моделирование завершено успешно!", vbInformation
End Sub

' === ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНЫЕ ПРОЦЕДУРЫ ===

Function ReadChannelCounts() As Object
    Dim dict As Object
    Set dict = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_CHANNELS)
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        ws.Name = SHEET_CHANNELS
        ws.Cells(1, 1).Value = "Элемент"
        ws.Cells(1, 2).Value = "Количество каналов"
        ws.Cells(2, 1).Value = "ВхI"
        ws.Cells(2, 2).Value = 2
        MsgBox "Создан лист 'Каналы'.", vbInformation
        Set ReadChannelCounts = dict
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, COL_CH_NAME).End(xlUp).Row
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        Dim elemName As String
        elemName = Trim(CStr(ws.Cells(i, COL_CH_NAME).Value))
        If elemName <> "" Then
            Dim chCount As Long
            If IsNumeric(ws.Cells(i, COL_CH_COUNT).Value) Then
                chCount = CLng(ws.Cells(i, COL_CH_COUNT).Value)
            Else
                chCount = 1
            End If
            If chCount < 1 Then chCount = 1
            dict.Add elemName, ch

### USER
Слово variant зарезервированное, нельзя его использовать

### ASSISTANT
Вы абсолютно правы! `Variant` — зарезервированное слово в VBA. Нужно переименовать все переменные и параметры с именем `variant`. Заменю их на `rtVar` (route variant).

Вот полный исправленный код:

```vba
Option Explicit

' === ТИПЫ ДАННЫХ ===
Type TElement
    Name As String
    DurationNoDelay As Double
    DurationWithDelay As Double
End Type

Type TRouteVariant
    Elements() As TElement
    ElementCount As Long
    ChannelName As String
End Type

Type TRequest
    ID As Long
    Name As String
    OriginalElements As String
    Variants() As TRouteVariant
    VariantCount As Long
    SelectedVariant As Long
    Priority As Double
    ArrivalTime As Double
    StartTime As Double
    EndTime As Double
    ActualDuration As Double
    DelayCausedBy As String
    SelectedChannel As String
    Status As String
End Type

' === КОНСТАНТЫ ===
Const COL_NAME As Integer = 1
Const COL_ARRIVAL As Integer = 2
Const COL_ELEMENTS As Integer = 3
Const COL_PRIORITY As Integer = 4

Const SHEET_DATA As String = "Данные"
Const SHEET_RESULT As String = "Результаты"

        Exit Sub
    End If

    lastRow = wsData.Cells(wsData.Rows.Count, COL_NAME).End(xlUp).Row

        MsgBox "Нет данных для моделирования.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ReDim requests(1 To lastRow - 1)

    For i = 2 To lastRow
        requests(i - 1).ID = i - 1
        requests(i - 1).Name = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_NAME).Value)

        elementsStr = CStr(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ELEMENTS).Value)
        requests(i - 1).OriginalElements = elementsStr
        
        ParseRouteVariants elementsStr, requests(i - 1)
        
        requests(i - 1).Priority = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_PRIORITY).Value)

            requests(i - 1).ArrivalTime = CDbl(wsData.Cells(i, COL_ARRIVAL).Value)
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка в строке " & i, vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
        
        requests(i - 1).ActualDuration = 0
        requests(i - 1).DelayCausedBy = ""
        requests(i - 1).SelectedChannel = ""
        requests(i - 1).StartTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).EndTime = -1
        requests(i - 1).SelectedVariant = -1
        requests(i - 1).Status = "New"
    Next i

    Dim currentTime As Double
    currentTime = 0
    
    Dim dictResources As Object
    Set dictResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    InitResources dictResources, dictChannelCounts

    Dim dictResTotalTime As Object
    Set dictResTotalTime = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary") 
    
    Dim dictResDelayCaused As Object
    Set dictResDelayCaused = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    Dim dictElementUsage As Object
    Set dictElementUsage = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")

    Dim allCompleted As Boolean
    Dim processed As Boolean
    Dim nextTime As Double
    Dim req As Long
    
    Do
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime <= currentTime Then
                requests(req).Status = "Waiting"
            End If
        Next req

        allCompleted = True
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status <> "Completed" Then
                allCompleted = False
                Exit For
            End If
        Next req
        If allCompleted Then Exit Do

        processed = False
        
        Dim waitingCount As Long
        For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
            If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then waitingCount = waitingCount + 1
        Next req

        If waitingCount > 0 Then
            Dim dictChannelSums As Object
            Set dictChannelSums = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            
            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim ch As String
                    If requests(req).VariantCount > 0 Then
                        ch = Trim(CStr(requests(req).Variants(0).ChannelName))
                    Else
                        ch = "Без_канала"
                    End If
                    
                    If ch = "" Then ch = "Без_канала"
                    
                    If Not dictChannelSums.Exists(ch) Then
                        dictChannelSums.Add ch, 0#
                    End If
                    dictChannelSums(ch) = dictChannelSums(ch) + (currentTime - (requests(req).ArrivalTime - requests(req).Priority))
                End If
            Next req

            If dictChannelSums.Count > 0 Then
                Dim channels() As Variant
                ReDim channels(1 To dictChannelSums.Count)
                Dim k As Long, key As Variant
                k = 1
                For Each key In dictChannelSums.Keys
                    channels(k) = key
                    k = k + 1
                Next key

                Dim j As Long, tempCh As Variant
                For i = 1 To UBound(channels) - 1
                    For j = i + 1 To UBound(channels)
                        If dictChannelSums(channels(j)) > dictChannelSums(channels(i)) Then
                            tempCh = channels(i)
                            channels(i) = channels(j)
                            channels(j) = tempCh
                        End If
                    Next j
                Next i

                For i = 1 To UBound(channels)
                    Dim bestCh As String
                    bestCh = channels(i)

                    Dim headReqIdx As Long
                    headReqIdx = -1
                    Dim minArr As Double
                    minArr = 999999999#

                    For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                        If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                            Dim reqChannel As String
                            If requests(req).VariantCount > 0 Then
                                reqChannel = Trim(CStr(requests(req).Variants(0).ChannelName))
                            Else
                                reqChannel = "Без_канала"
                            End If
                            
                            If reqChannel = bestCh Then
                                If requests(req).ArrivalTime < minArr Then
                                    minArr = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                                    headReqIdx = req
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next req

                    If headReqIdx <> -1 Then
                        Dim bestVarIdx As Long
                        Dim bestStartTime As Double
                        bestVarIdx = -1
                        bestStartTime = 999999999#
                        
                        Dim v As Long
                        For v = 0 To requests(headReqIdx).VariantCount - 1
                            Dim varStartTime As Double
                            varStartTime = GetVarStartTime(requests(headReqIdx), v, currentTime, dictResources)
                            
                            If varStartTime < bestStartTime Then
                                bestStartTime = varStartTime
                                bestVarIdx = v
                            End If
                        Next v
                        
                        If bestVarIdx >= 0 Then
                            requests(headReqIdx).StartTime = bestStartTime
                            requests(headReqIdx).SelectedVariant = bestVarIdx
                            
                            Dim isDelayed As Boolean
                            isDelayed = (requests(headReqIdx).StartTime > requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
                            
                            Dim channelDuration As Double
                            If isDelayed Then
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVarIdx).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
                            Else
                                channelDuration = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVarIdx).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
                            End If
                            
                            requests(headReqIdx).ActualDuration = channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).EndTime = bestStartTime + channelDuration
                            requests(headReqIdx).Status = "Completed"
                            
                            If isDelayed Then
                                Dim bottleneckRes As String
                                bottleneckRes = requests(headReqIdx).Variants(bestVarIdx).ChannelName
                                
                                Dim delayAmount As Double
                                delayAmount = requests(headReqIdx).StartTime - requests(headReqIdx).ArrivalTime
                                
                                If Not dictResDelayCaused.Exists(bottleneckRes) Then
                                    dictResDelayCaused.Add bottleneckRes, 0#
                                End If
                                dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) = dictResDelayCaused(bottleneckRes) + delayAmount
                                
                                requests(headReqIdx).DelayCausedBy = bottleneckRes
                            End If
                            
                            UpdateResources requests(headReqIdx), dictResources, dictResTotalTime, dictElementUsage, dictChannelCounts
                            
                            processed = True
                            Exit For 
                        End If
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
        End If

        If Not processed Then
            nextTime = 999999999#

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "New" And requests(req).ArrivalTime > currentTime Then
                    If requests(req).ArrivalTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = requests(req).ArrivalTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            For req = 1 To UBound(requests)
                If requests(req).Status = "Waiting" Then
                    Dim relTime As Double
                    relTime = 999999999#
                    
                    For v = 0 To requests(req).VariantCount - 1
                        Dim varRelTime As Double
                        varRelTime = GetEarliestVarRelease(requests(req), v, currentTime, dictResources)
                        If varRelTime < relTime Then
                            relTime = varRelTime
                        End If
                    Next v
                    
                    If relTime > currentTime And relTime < nextTime Then
                        nextTime = relTime
                    End If
                End If
            Next req

            If nextTime = 999999999# Then Exit Do
            
            currentTime = nextTime
        End If
    Loop

    OutputResults requests, dictResTotalTime, dictResDelayCaused, dictElementUsage
    
    MsgBox "Моделирование завершено успешно!", vbInformation
End Sub

' === ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНЫЕ ПРОЦЕДУРЫ ===

Function ReadChannelCounts() As Object
    Dim dict As Object
    Set dict = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_CHANNELS)
    On Error GoTo 0
    
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        ws.Name = SHEET_CHANNELS
        ws.Cells(1, 1).Value = "Элемент"
        ws.Cells(1, 2).Value = "Количество каналов"
        ws.Cells(2, 1).Value = "ВхI"
        ws.Cells(2, 2).Value = 2
        MsgBox "Создан лист 'Каналы'.", vbInformation
        Set ReadChannelCounts = dict
        Exit Function
    End If
    
    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.Rows.Count, COL_CH_NAME).End(xlUp).Row
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 2 To lastRow
        Dim elemName As String
        elemName = Trim(CStr(ws.Cells(i, COL_CH_NAME).Value))
        If elemName <> "" Then
            Dim chCount As Long
            If IsNumeric(ws.Cells(i, COL_CH_COUNT).Value) Then
                chCount = CLng(ws.Cells(i, COL_CH_COUNT).Value)
            Else
                chCount = 1
            End If
            If chCount < 1 Then chCount = 1
            dict.Add elemName, chCount
        End If
    Next i
    
    Set ReadChannelCounts = dict
End Function

Sub InitResources(dictRes As Object, dictChannelCounts As Object)
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In dictChannelCounts.Keys
        Dim chCount As Long
        chCount = dictChannelCounts(key)
        
        Dim times() As Double
        ReDim times(0 To chCount - 1)
        Dim i As Long
        For i = 0 To chCount - 1
            times(i) = 0#
        Next i
        
        dictRes.Add key, times
    Next key
End Sub

Sub ParseRouteVariants(elementsStr As String, ByRef req As TRequest)
    Dim variantsArr() As String
    variantsArr = Split(elementsStr, ";")
    
    Dim varCount As Long
    varCount = UBound(variantsArr) - LBound(variantsArr) + 1
    
    ReDim req.Variants(0 To varCount - 1)
    req.VariantCount = varCount
    
    Dim v As Long
    For v = LBound(variantsArr) To UBound(variantsArr)
        Dim varStr As String
        varStr = Trim(variantsArr(v))
        
        ParseRouteVar varStr, req.Variants(v)
    Next v
End Sub

Sub ParseRouteVar(varStr As String, ByRef rtVar As TRouteVariant)
    Dim arr() As String
    arr = Split(varStr, ",")
    
    Dim count As Long
    count = UBound(arr) - LBound(arr) + 1
    
    ReDim rtVar.Elements(0 To count - 1)
    rtVar.ElementCount = count
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr)
        Dim part As String
        part = Trim(arr(i))
        
        Dim parts() As String
        parts = Split(part, ":")
        
        If UBound(parts) >= 2 Then
            rtVar.Elements(i).Name = Trim(parts(0))
            rtVar.Elements(i).DurationNoDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(1)))
            rtVar.Elements(i).DurationWithDelay = CDbl(Trim(parts(2)))
        Else
            MsgBox "Ошибка парсинга элемента: " & part, vbCritical
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next i
    
    If count > 0 Then
        rtVar.ChannelName = rtVar.Elements(0).Name
    Else
        rtVar.ChannelName = "Без_канала"
    End If
End Sub

Function GetVarStartTime(req As TRequest, varIdx As Long, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    Dim earliestStart As Double
    earliestStart = currentTime
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(varIdx).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(varIdx).Elements(i).Name
        
        If dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim times() As Double
            times = dictRes(resName)
            
            Dim minFreeTime As Double
            minFreeTime = 999999999#
            Dim j As Long
            For j = 0 To UBound(times)
                If times(j) < minFreeTime Then
                    minFreeTime = times(j)
                End If
            Next j
            
            If minFreeTime > earliestStart Then
                earliestStart = minFreeTime
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    
    GetVarStartTime = earliestStart
End Function

Function GetEarliestVarRelease(req As TRequest, varIdx As Long, currentTime As Double, dictRes As Object) As Double
    Dim earliest As Double
    earliest = 999999999#
    
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(varIdx).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(varIdx).Elements(i).Name
        
        If dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim times() As Double
            times = dictRes(resName)
            
            Dim j As Long
            For j = 0 To UBound(times)
                If times(j) > currentTime And times(j) < earliest Then
                    earliest = times(j)
                End If
            Next j
        End If
    Next i
    
    GetEarliestVarRelease = earliest
End Function

Sub UpdateResources(req As TRequest, dictRes As Object, dictResTotal As Object, dictElementUsage As Object, dictChannelCounts As Object)
    If req.SelectedVariant < 0 Then Exit Sub
    
    Dim i As Long
    
    Dim duration As Double
    Dim isDelayed As Boolean
    isDelayed = (req.StartTime > req.ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
    
    If isDelayed Then
        duration = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
    Else
        duration = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
    End If
    
    Dim channelInfo As String
    channelInfo = ""
    
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(i).Name
        
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim times(0) As Double
            times(0) = 0#
            dictRes.Add resName, times
        End If
        
        Dim times() As Double
        times = dictRes(resName)
        
        Dim bestChannel As Long
        bestChannel = 0
        Dim minTime As Double
        minTime = times(0)
        
        Dim j As Long
        For j = 1 To UBound(times)
            If times(j) < minTime Then
                minTime = times(j)
                bestChannel = j
            End If
        Next j
        
        times(bestChannel) = req.EndTime
        dictRes(resName) = times
        
        If channelInfo = "" Then
            channelInfo = resName & "(канал " & (bestChannel + 1) & ")"
        Else
            channelInfo = channelInfo & ", " & resName & "(канал " & (bestChannel + 1) & ")"
        End If
        
        If Not dictResTotal.Exists(resName) Then
            dictResTotal.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        dictResTotal(resName) = dictResTotal(resName) + duration
        
        If Not dictElementUsage.Exists(resName) Then
            dictElementUsage.Add resName, CreateObject("System.Collections.ArrayList")
        End If
        
        Dim usageList As Object
        Set usageList = dictElementUsage(resName)
        
        Dim usageEntry As String
        usageEntry = "(""" & req.Name & """ " & req.StartTime & " " & req.EndTime & " к" & (bestChannel + 1) & ")"
        usageList.Add usageEntry
    Next i
    
    req.SelectedChannel = channelInfo
End Sub

Sub OutputResults(requests() As TRequest, dictResTotal As Object, dictResDelay As Object, dictElementUsage As Object)
    Dim wsRes As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets(SHEET_RESULT)
    If wsRes Is Nothing Then
        Set wsRes = ThisWorkbook.Sheets.Add
        wsRes.Name = SHEET_RESULT
    Else
        wsRes.Cells.Clear
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0

    wsRes.Cells(1, 1).Value = "Наименование"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 2).Value = "Элементы (варианты)"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 3).Value = "Выбранный вариант"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 4).Value = "Занятые каналы"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 5).Value = "Время поступл."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 6).Value = "Начало обслуж."
    wsRes.Cells(1, 7).Value = "Время окончания"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 8).Value = "Факт. длительность"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 9).Value = "Задержка"
    wsRes.Cells(1, 10).Value = "Элементы, вызвавшие задержку"
    
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To UBound(requests)
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 1).Value = requests(r).Name
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 2).Value = requests(r).OriginalElements
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 3).Value = requests(r).SelectedVariant + 1
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 4).Value = requests(r).SelectedChannel
        wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 5).Value = requests(r).ArrivalTime
        
        If requests(r).StartTime >= 0 Then
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 6).Value = requests(r).StartTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 7).Value = requests(r).EndTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 8).Value = requests(r).ActualDuration
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 9).Value = requests(r).StartTime - requests(r).ArrivalTime
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 10).Value = requests(r).DelayCausedBy
        Else
            wsRes.Cells(r + 1, 6).Value = "Ошибка"
        End If
    Next r
    
    With wsRes.Range("A1:J" & UBound(requests) + 1)
        .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
        .Columns.AutoFit
    End With
    wsRes.Range("A1:J1").Font.Bold = True
    wsRes.Range("A1:J1").Interior.Color = RGB(200, 220, 240)

    Dim startRowRes As Long
    startRowRes = UBound(requests) + 4
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 1).Value = "Элемент"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 2).Value = "Суммарное время занятости"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 3).Value = "Суммарная задержка, вызванная элементом"
    wsRes.Cells(startRowRes, 4).Value = "Список операций"
    
    Dim allResources As Object
    Set allResources = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim key As Variant
    For Each key In dictResTotal.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key
    For Each key In dictResDelay.Keys
        If Not allResources.Exists(key) Then allResources.Add key, 0
    Next key

    Dim resCount As Long
    resCount = allResources.Count
    
    If resCount > 0 Then
        Dim resNames() As String
        Dim resDelays() As Double
        ReDim resNames(1 To resCount)
        ReDim resDelays(1 To resCount)
        
        Dim idx As Long
        idx = 1
        For Each key In allResources.Keys
            resNames(idx) = key
            If dictResDelay.Exists(key) Then
                resDelays(idx) = dictResDelay(key)
            Else
                resDelays(idx) = 0
            End If
            idx = idx + 1
        Next key
        
        Dim m As Long, n As Long
        Dim tempName As String, tempDelay As Double
        For m = 1 To resCount - 1
            For n = m + 1 To resCount
                If resDelays(n) > resDelays(m) Then
                    tempDelay = resDelays(m)
                    resDelays(m) = resDelays(n)
                    resDelays(n) = tempDelay
                    
                    tempName = resNames(m)
                    resNames(m) = resNames(n)
                    resNames(n) = tempName
                End If
            Next n
        Next m
        
        Dim rowIdx As Long
        rowIdx = startRowRes + 1
        For idx = 1 To resCount
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 1).Value = resNames(idx)
            
            Dim totalBusy As Double
            totalBusy = 0
            If dictResTotal.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then totalBusy = dictResTotal(resNames(idx))
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 2).Value = totalBusy
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 3).Value = resDelays(idx)
            
            Dim usageStr As String
            usageStr = """" & resNames(idx) & """ "
            
            If dictElementUsage.Exists(resNames(idx)) Then
                Dim usageList As Object
                Set usageList = dictElementUsage(resNames(idx))
                Dim entry As Variant
                For Each entry In usageList
                    usageStr = usageStr & entry & " "
                Next entry
            End If
            
            wsRes.Cells(rowIdx, 4).Value = Trim(usageStr)
            
            rowIdx = rowIdx + 1
        Next idx
        
        With wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & (rowIdx - 1))
            .Borders.LineStyle = xlContinuous
            .Columns.AutoFit
        End With
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & startRowRes).Font.Bold = True
        wsRes.Range("A" & startRowRes & ":D" & startRowRes).Interior.Color = RGB(220, 240, 200)
    End If
    
    wsRes.Columns.AutoFit
End Sub
```

### Что изменено:

Все вхождения `variant` как имени переменной/параметра заменены:

| Было | Стало |
|------|-------|
| `variant As TRouteVariant` | `rtVar As TRouteVariant` |
| `ParseVariant` | `ParseRouteVar` |
| `GetVariantStartTime` | `GetVarStartTime` |
| `GetEarliestVariantRelease` | `GetEarliestVarRelease` |
| `variantStr` | `varStr` |
| `variantCount` | `varCount` |
| `bestVariant` | `bestVarIdx` |
| `variantStartTime` | `varStartTime` |
| `variantRelTime` | `varRelTime` |
| `varIdx` (параметр) | оставлен, т.к. это не `variant` |

Тип `TRouteVariant` оставлен без изменений — составные имена с `Variant` внутри (как `TRouteVariant`) VBA разрешает использовать. Зарезервировано только одиночное слово `Variant`.

### USER


### ASSISTANT
Ошибка "Duplicate declaration in current scope" возникает потому, что переменная `times` объявляется дважды в одном блоке кода:

1. Первый раз: `Dim times(0) As Double` (внутри `If Not dictRes.Exists`)
2. Второй раз: `Dim times() As Double` (после `End If`)

Нужно объявить переменную `times` только один раз. Вот исправленная процедура `UpdateResources`:

```vba
Sub UpdateResources(req As TRequest, dictRes As Object, dictResTotal As Object, dictElementUsage As Object, dictChannelCounts As Object)
    If req.SelectedVariant < 0 Then Exit Sub
    
    Dim i As Long
    
    Dim duration As Double
    Dim isDelayed As Boolean
    isDelayed = (req.StartTime > req.ArrivalTime + 0.0001)
    
    If isDelayed Then
        duration = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(0).DurationWithDelay
    Else
        duration = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(0).DurationNoDelay
    End If
    
    Dim channelInfo As String
    channelInfo = ""
    
    For i = 0 To req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).ElementCount - 1
        Dim resName As String
        resName = req.Variants(req.SelectedVariant).Elements(i).Name
        
        ' Если ресурс не существует, создаем его с 1 каналом
        If Not dictRes.Exists(resName) Then
            Dim newTimes(0) As Double
            newTimes(0) = 0#
            dictRes.Add resName, newTimes
        End If
        
        ' Получаем массив времени освобождения каналов
        Dim timesArr As Variant
        timesArr = dictRes(resName)
        
        ' Находим канал с минимальным временем освобождения
        Dim bestChannel As Long
        bestChannel = 0
        Dim minTime As Double
        minTime = timesArr(0)
        
        Dim j As Long
        For j = 1 To UBound(timesArr)
            If timesArr(j) < minTime Then
                minTime = timesArr(j)
                bestChannel = j
            End If
        Next j
        
        ' Занимаем этот канал
        timesArr(bestChannel) = req.EndTime
        dictRes(resName) = timesArr
        
        ' Формируем информацию о канале
        If channelInfo = "" Then
            channelInfo = resName & "(канал " & (bestChannel + 1) & ")"
        Else
            channelInfo = channelInfo & ", " & resName & "(канал " & (bestChannel + 1) & ")"
        End If
        
        ' Статистика по времени занятости
        If Not dictResTotal.Exists(resName) Then
            dictResTotal.Add resName, 0#
        End If
        dictResTotal(resName) = dictResTotal(resName) + duration
        
        ' Список использования
        If Not dictElementUsage.Exists(resName) Then
            dictElementUsage.Add resName, CreateObject("System.Collections.ArrayList")
        End If
        
        Dim usageList As Object
        Set usageList = dictElementUsage(resName)
        
        Dim usageEntry As String
        usageEntry = "(""" & req.Name & """ " & req.StartTime & " " & req.EndTime & " к" & (bestChannel + 1) & ")"
        usageList.Add usageEntry
    Next i
    
    req.SelectedChannel = channelInfo
End Sub
```

### Что исправлено:

1. **Убрано дублирование объявления `times`**:
   - Внутри `If` создается `newTimes(0)` и добавляется в словарь
   - После `End If` используется `timesArr As Variant` для получения массива из словаря

2. **Использование `Variant`** вместо `Double()` для получения массива из словаря — это стандартный подход в VBA при работе с массивами в `Dictionary`.

Теперь компиляция должна пройти успешно!

### USER
код целиком

### ASSISTANT
```vba
Option Explicit

' === ТИПЫ ДАННЫХ ===
Type TElement
    Name As String
    DurationNoDelay As Double
    DurationWithDelay As Double
End Type

Type TRouteVariant
    Elements() As TElement
    ElementCount As Long
    ChannelName As String
End Type

Type TRequest
    ID As Long
    Name As String
    OriginalElements As String
